TL;DR: 本文提出PatchSTG，一种基于分块的空时图Transformer，通过将不规则传感器网络划分为平衡且保持局部性的空间块，结合双注意力编码器（块内注意力捕捉局部交互，块间注意力建模全局依赖），将计算复杂度从二次降至近线性，在真实交通数据集上实现高效且稳定的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测模型在处理实际中传感器分布不均匀、大规模时空依赖计算成本高的问题时，图基和注意力基模型的可扩展性和有效性受限。

Method: 提出分块空时Transformer (PatchSTG)：1) 基于地理信息进行层次化空间划分，将传感器分入平衡且保持局部性的块；2) 双注意力编码器交替进行块内注意力（局部交互）和块间注意力（全局依赖），将复杂度从二次降至近线性。

Result: 在罗德岛真实交通数据及大规模数据集上，PatchSTG在不同预测时域上均实现了稳定且具有竞争力的预测性能，同时显著提升了计算效率；消融实验验证了空间划分和双注意力的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于分块的时空建模为不规则空间设置下的交通预测提供了可扩展且有效的框架。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is a fundamental component of intelligent transportation systems, yet remains challenging in real-world settings due to irregular sensor distributions and the high computational cost of modeling large-scale spatiotemporal dependencies. In practical traffic networks, sensors are unevenly distributed across regions, leading to non-uniform spatial structures that limit the effectiveness and scalability of existing graph-based and attention-based models. To address these challenges, we propose PatchSTG, a patch-based spatiotemporal graph Transformer designed for efficient forecasting on irregular sensor networks. The key idea is to introduce a hierarchical spatial representation that partitions sensors into balanced, locality-preserving patches based on geographic information. On top of this structure, a dual attention encoder alternates between intra-patch attention for capturing local interactions and inter-patch attention for modeling global dependencies, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to near-linear scaling. We evaluate PatchSTG on real-world traffic data from Rhode Island and additional large-scale datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model achieves stable and competitive forecasting performance across multiple horizons, while significantly improving computational efficiency. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of spatial partitioning and dual attention in capturing both local and long-range traffic dynamics. These results suggest that patch-based spatiotemporal modeling provides a scalable and effective framework for traffic forecasting under irregular spatial settings.

</details>


### [28] [Rank Collapse, Fixed Points, and the Renormalization Group Structure of MLP Residual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10324)
*Parviz Haggi-Mani,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过残差MLP在合成马尔可夫链上的掩码预测任务，首次定量验证了深度神经网络前向传播与重正化群流之间的类比：训练后残差流有效秩随深度单调下降（短相关链）而选择性保留相关自由度，层间核漂移集中在少数几个转变处，其余部分接近不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中神经网络前向传播与RG流的类比停留在定性描述层面，缺乏可测量的RG序参量、对输入分布的系统控制以及可验证的定量预测。

Method: 在纯MLP残差堆叠架构上，对具有已知谱性质的合成马尔可夫链序列进行掩码词元预测训练，通过残差流有效秩、层间核漂移等指标进行定量分析。

Result: (i)训练后残差流有效秩随深度单调下降；(ii)该秩坍缩具有选择性——短相关链(相关长度≈1)发生坍缩，长相关链(≈7)则否；(iii)层间核漂移集中在一两个特定转变处，其余部分接近不动点。

Conclusion: MLP残差网络实现了一种由输入分布谱结构控制的选择性粗粒化过程，首次提供了位置级别的定量证据。

Abstract: The analogy between deep neural network forward passes and renormalization group (RG) flows has been repeatedly noted in the literature, but existing treatments remain qualitative: depth is described as a coarse-graining scale, attention is likened to a partition function, and representations are said to flow toward fixed points. No existing work has defined a measurable RG order parameter, tested it under controlled variation of the input distribution, or made quantitative predictions that are empirically verified. We study the simplest architecture for which the analogy is tractable: a pure MLP residual stack trained on masked token prediction over synthetic Markov chain sequences with known spectral properties. We report three findings. (i) The effective rank of the residual stream decreases monotonically with depth after training, consistent with progressive integration of irrelevant degrees of freedom. (ii) This rank collapse is selective: it occurs for chains with short correlation length approximately 1 but is absent for chains with long correlation length approximately 7, measured at the position level to control for mean-pooling artifacts. The network preserves exactly the degrees of freedom relevant to the prediction task, the content of the RG relevance criterion. (iii) Inter-layer kernel drift is concentrated at one or two specific transitions, with the remainder of the network near a fixed point, consistent with a discrete fixed-point plateau. Together these findings constitute the first quantitative, position-level evidence that MLP residual networks implement a selective coarse-graining procedure governed by the spectral structure of the input distribution.

</details>


### [29] [Rotate2Think: Geometric Priming via Orthogonal Rotation to Improve Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09873)
*Aditya Sharma,Christopher J. Pal,Amal Zouaq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现推理模型的思考嵌入与输入嵌入高度聚集且方向不同，提出无需训练的Rotate2Think方法，通过正交Procrustes分析估计从输入到思考的旋转，在推理时注入合成思考向量，在32个模型-基准配置中的30个上提升了数学、科学和代码任务的准确率，并零样本泛化到多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在生成中间推理步骤时表现优异，但其隐藏表示在思考过程中的内部结构尚不清楚——模型的隐藏表示在思考时与输入嵌入有何不同，以及能否利用这种结构在推理时激发更强的推理能力。

Method: 通过观察输入嵌入和思考嵌入（分别对提示和推理轨迹进行均值池化的最后一层隐藏状态）均呈现极高锥度（所有向量紧密聚集在单一均值方向周围），且两者方向非共线、占据几何上不同的区域。基于此提出Rotate2Think：将输入到思考的转变建模为旋转问题，通过正交Procrustes分析从少量正确求解示例中估计旋转矩阵，在推理时在思考分隔符之间注入合成的思考向量，作为推理轨迹起始处的几何引导。

Result: 在多个基准和模型家族上评估，Rotate2Think在32个模型-基准配置中的30个上提升了数学、科学和代码任务的准确率，并零样本泛化到MATH-Vision多模态推理任务。

Conclusion: 推理模型的思考嵌入与输入嵌入在表示空间中存在几何可分的旋转关系，利用这一结构无需训练即可提升推理能力，该方法简单有效且具有跨任务、跨模态的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reasoning models achieve strong performance on challenging tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning traces before producing a final answer. Yet the internal structure of representation space when reasoning remains poorly understood: how do a model's hidden representations differ during thinking versus the embeddings of the input prompt, and can this structure be exploited to elicit stronger reasoning at inference time? We show that both input embeddings and thinking embeddings (mean-pooled last-layer hidden states over the prompt and reasoning trace, respectively) exhibit extremely high conicity, with all vectors clustering tightly around a single mean direction. Crucially, these mean input and thinking directions are non-collinear, with thinking embeddings occupying a geometrically distinct region of embedding space across many different models and benchmark tasks. This observation motivates casting the input-to-thinking transition as a rotation problem admitting a closed-form solution via orthogonal Procrustes analysis. We propose Rotate2Think, a training-free method that estimates this rotation from a small set of correctly solved examples and injects the resulting synthetic thinking vector between thinking delimiters at inference time, providing a geometric primer at the onset of the reasoning trace. Evaluated across multiple benchmarks and model families, Rotate2Think improves accuracy in 30 of 32 model-benchmark configurations across mathematics, science, and code tasks, and generalizes zero-shot to multimodal reasoning on MATH-Vision.

</details>


### [30] [Advancing the State-of-the-Art in Empirical Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10481)
*Nicole Mitchell,Galen Andrew,Arun Ganesh,Brendan McMahan,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过高温采样生成合成金丝雀数据来审计大语言模型微调中的记忆化隐私风险，并引入基于辅助模型的合成数据审计方法，系统研究了模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调大语言模型可能过度记忆训练样本，引发隐私泄露风险。现有经验性隐私审计方法需要设计混合在隐私敏感训练数据中的"金丝雀"样本，这一过程具有挑战性。

Method: (1) 通过高温采样(T≥0.8)从LLM生成合成金丝雀，并设计针对隐私敏感数据的提示模板，使其成为高影响离群点；(2) 利用合成数据微调辅助模型，通过审计辅助模型来估计原始合成数据的隐私泄露；(3) 系统研究模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互效应。

Result: 合成金丝雀具有高可识别性，能实现强审计效果；基于辅助模型的合成数据审计方法能有效估计隐私泄露风险；模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化存在交互影响。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了非私有的、可检查的、可重复插入的金丝雀样本，在保护真实数据隐私的同时实现了强审计能力，为LLM隐私审计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) can exhibit problematic memorization of individual training examples. Empirical privacy auditing (EPA) quantifies this risk by measuring realistic data leakage on membership inference (MI) or reconstruction attacks. A key challenge in EPA is designing ``canary'' examples that are mixed with the privacy-sensitive training data. We propose generating synthetic canaries via high-temperature sampling ($T \geq 0.8$) from LLMs, using prompts tailored to the privacy-sensitive training data. These canaries act as high-influence outliers, ensuring high identifiability and hence strong audits. Further, since the canaries are themselves non-private, they are inspectable and can be inserted with repetition without jeopardizing the privacy of the real data. An important use of models fine-tuned on privacy-sensitive data is the generation of synthetic data. This also comes with privacy risk. We introduce a powerful synthetic data audit based on fine-tuning an auxiliary model on the synthetic data. Auditing the auxiliary model for the original canaries then provides a strong estimate of the privacy leakage through the synthetic data. Finally, leveraging our strong auditing methodologies, we perform a systematic investigation into the interacting effects of model capacity and canary entropy on memorization.

</details>


### [31] [Structured Adaptive Tensor Prediction for Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10085)
*Zhen Qin,Yang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对流式矩阵值时间序列预测问题，提出了自适应张量回归框架（包括MoM和ToM两种形式），开发了SGD在线学习算法，证明了将多响应堆叠为高阶张量（ToM）在稳态误差和去噪能力上优于直接建模矩阵输出的方法（MoM），并给出了在稀疏、低秩及联合稀疏-低秩结构下的固定时间恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵值时间序列方法主要针对静态设置，缺乏对流式和时变环境的适应性；自适应滤波技术多局限于标量或向量数据，矩阵值时间序列的自适应预测问题尚未被充分理解。

Method: 提出自适应张量回归框架，包含Matrix-on-Matrix（MoM）和Tensor-on-Matrix（ToM）两种形式；采用随机梯度下降（SGD）算法进行在线学习；将多个时间点的响应堆叠成高阶张量以利用时序结构。

Result: ToM在稳态误差和去噪能力上优于MoM；建立了ToM在稀疏、低秩及联合稀疏-低秩结构下的固定时间恢复保证；刻画了SGD在时变动态下的跟踪行为。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效填补了矩阵值时间序列自适应预测的空白，ToM模型因其优越性能值得重点关注，SGD算法在时变环境下具有可理论保证的跟踪能力。

Abstract: Matrix-valued time series arise in a wide range of applications, such as spatio-temporal data from medical imaging and geophysics. Existing methods are mainly designed for static settings and lack adaptability to streaming and time-varying environments. Adaptive filtering techniques have also been largely limited to data with scalar or vector values, leaving adaptive forecasting for matrix-valued time series inadequately understood. To bridge these gaps, we develop an adaptive tensor regression framework that includes Matrix-on-Matrix (MoM) and Tensor-on-Matrix (ToM) formulations for streaming matrix-valued prediction. The two formulations differ in whether to directly model matrix-valued outputs or to exploit temporal structure via higher-order tensor representations. For the proposed tensor regression framework, we develop stochastic gradient descent (SGD) algorithms for online learning. We show that stacking multiple responses across time into higher-order tensors improves performance; in particular, the ToM achieves lower steady-state error and stronger denoising capability than MoM, motivating our focus on the ToM model. We further characterize the tracking behavior of SGD under time-varying dynamics. From a statistical perspective, we establish fixed-time recovery guarantees for ToM under general low-dimensional structures, including sparsity, low-rankness, and their joint sparselow-rank models.

</details>


### [32] [SPACR: Single-Pass Adaptive Training of Uncertainty-Aware Conformal Regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10734)
*Soundouss Messoudi,Sylvain Rousseau,Sébastien Destercke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPACR方法，通过可微损失函数直接训练不确定性感知的回归模型，联合优化预测区间的有效性和效率，无需批分割或预设置信水平，在多个数据集上比标准CP和DOICR方法产生更窄的区间、更好的覆盖-效率权衡，且计算成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形预测（CP）通常事后应用于已训练模型，导致模型训练与共形目标（产生高效/窄区间）不一致，且现有方法如DOICR需要针对不同置信水平重新训练，计算成本高。

Method: 提出SPACR（单遍自适应共形回归器），设计可微损失函数直接训练不确定性感知的回归器，联合优化预测区间的效率（窄度）和有效性（覆盖），训练过程中无需批分割或预设置信水平，单个模型即可在推理时生成多个置信水平下的有效预测区间。

Result: 在多个不同数据集上的实验表明，SPACR相比标准CP和DOICR方法始终产生更紧致的预测区间，实现更好的覆盖-效率权衡，同时计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: SPACR通过将共形目标融入训练过程，有效解决了传统CP事后应用的不匹配问题，单个模型支持多置信水平推理，在性能和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Conformal Prediction (CP) provides robust uncertainty guarantees for predictive models, but is typically applied post hoc, which misaligns model training with the conformal goal of producing efficient (i.e, narrow) intervals. We propose SPACR (Single-Pass Adaptive Conformal Regressor), a novel method for directly training uncertainty-aware regressors within a differentiable loss. SPACR jointly optimizes efficiency and validity without batch-splitting or a predefined confidence levels during training. As a result, a single SPACR model yields valid prediction intervals at multiple confidence levels during inference, avoiding the costly retraining required by methods like DOICR. Experiments on diverse datasets show that SPACR consistently gives tighter intervals and better coverage-efficiency trade-offs compared to standard CP and DOICR, while significantly reducing computational costs.

</details>


### [33] [Calibrating Overconfidence Without Sacrificing Confidence: Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09876)
*Ke Li,Chongzhe Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Feng Liu,Qunli Zhang,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出探针条件化头部干预（PCHI）方法，通过冻结探针检测高置信但错误的回答，并在推理时条件性地缩放注意力头输出，在减少错误高置信的同时仅轻微损害正确置信。在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上，将82.2%的错误-yes置信读头转换为no，ECE从21.9%降至9.2%，仅损害5.1%的正确-yes读头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常对错误答案表达高置信度，现有校准方法全局或分数级操作虽能降低不合理置信，但也会侵蚀正确答案的合理置信。需要一种能选择性降低错误高置信而不损害正确置信的方法。

Method: PCHI（Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention）：使用冻结探针检测可能错误但高置信的响应，然后在置信度生成期间条件性地缩放下游注意力头输出。包含读头token干预和上游置信模板token联合干预两种方式。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上：读头token PCHI将82.2%的错误-yes置信读头转换为no；联合干预将ECE从21.9%降至9.2%，仅损害5.1%的正确-yes读头。在Gemma3-4B上读头token效果类似，但上游干预较弱且更依赖掩码选择。

Conclusion: 通过条件性内部干预可以选择性降低语言模型的口头过度置信，部分解耦了抑制不合理置信与损失合理置信之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Large language models often express high confidence in answers that are wrong. Standard calibration remedies typically act globally or at the score level, reducing unwarranted confidence but also risking erosion of warranted confidence on correct answers. We introduce Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI), an inference-time method that uses a frozen probe to detect likely wrong-but-confident responses and conditionally rescales downstream attention-head outputs during confidence generation. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct solving OpenMathInstruct problems with a structured binary confidence field, readout-token PCHI converts 82.2% of originally wrong-yes confidence readouts to $\texttt{no}$, while a joint intervention across upstream confidence-template tokens reduces ECE from 21.9% to 9.2% and damages only 5.1% of originally correct-yes readouts. The readout-token effect also appears on Gemma3-4B, though upstream interventions are weaker and more mask-dependent. These results show that verbalized overconfidence can be selectively reduced through conditionally applied internal intervention, partially decoupling the suppression of unwarranted confidence from the loss of warranted confidence.

</details>


### [34] [Baseline-Free Policy Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10321)
*Carlos S. Sepúlveda,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了GRPO（组相对策略优化）在神经组合优化中的应用，发现它能够避免REINFORCE在TSP-100上的训练崩溃，在匹配梯度更新次数下达到POMO 2%以内的解质量，且无需外部基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REINFORCE with rollout baseline在解决更难实例时存在结构脆弱性：差的基线产生噪声梯度估计，导致训练不稳定甚至崩溃。

Method: 在RL4CO框架下，对TSP和CVRP基准上五种RL算法进行控制实验，重点比较GRPO（通过组内轨迹归一化优势来消除基线）与REINFORCE、POMO、P3O等算法。

Result: (i) GRPO避免了REINFORCE在TSP-100上从成本9.8骤降至52.1的训练崩溃；(ii) 在相同梯度更新次数下，GRPO解质量在POMO的2%以内；(iii) P3O在TSP上表现有竞争力，但在CVRP上变异性更高。

Conclusion: GRPO是一种有前景的无基线替代方案，特别适用于基线依赖训练变得脆弱的场景。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) trains autoregressive policies to solve routing problems. The standard training algorithm, REINFORCE with a rollout baseline, requires maintaining and periodically updating a frozen copy of the policy for variance reduction. This baseline introduces a structural vulnerability: on harder instances, a poor baseline produces noisy gradient estimates that can destabilize training. We evaluate Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an algorithm from large language model alignment that eliminates the baseline entirely by normalizing advantages within groups of sampled trajectories. In a controlled comparison of five RL algorithms on TSP and CVRP benchmarks within the RL4CO framework, we find that: (i) GRPO avoids the training collapse observed with REINFORCE on TSP-100, where performance degrades from cost 9.8 to 52.1 immediately after the warmup phase and does not recover under extended training; (ii) at matched gradient updates, GRPO achieves solution quality within 2% of POMO, a strong AM-based multi-start baseline, while requiring no external baseline; and (iii) P3O, a pairwise preference algorithm also from the alignment literature, is competitive on TSP but shows higher variability on CVRP. These results identify GRPO as a promising baseline-free alternative for NCO, particularly in settings where baseline-dependent training becomes fragile.

</details>


### [35] [Conservation Laws from Data Symmetry in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10913)
*Jakob Galley,Vahid Shahverdi,Axel Flinth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了训练数据的固有对称性在神经网络梯度流训练中是否会导致守恒量。证明对于解析且非多项式的损失函数，数据对称性通常不会产生额外的运动积分；但对于均方误差损失，数据增强在某些情况下会产生额外的守恒量。作者利用"可张量化网络"框架描述了这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究训练数据的对称性是否会在神经网络梯度流训练中诱导出守恒量，从而理解数据增强背后的理论机制。

Method: 基于损失函数的解析性质（解析且非多项式 vs 均方误差损失）进行分析，引入"可张量化网络"框架来分离参数和输入的依赖关系。

Result: 解析非多项式损失下数据对称性不产生额外守恒量；MSE损失下数据增强可能产生额外守恒量。

Conclusion: 数据对称性对梯度流动力学的影响取决于损失函数的类型，MSE损失下可张量化网络能解释数据增强带来的守恒现象。

Abstract: We explore whether intrinsic symmetries of the training data lead to conserved quantities during gradient-flow training of neural networks. Under the assumption that the loss function is analytic and non-polynomial, we prove that data symmetries generically do not induce any additional integrals of motion. For mean squared error (MSE) loss, on the other hand, there are situations in which data augmentation yields extra conserved quantities. We build a framework, utilizing \emph{tensorizable networks} to describe this phenomenon. Tensorizable networks are a family of architectures whose dependence on parameters and inputs can be separated using an intermediate representation. They include linear and polynomial networks, as well as Lightning Attention.

</details>


### [36] [Streaming Knowledge Compilation: Proactive Materiality-Scored Pinning for Time-Evolving LLM Wikis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09877)
*Juan M. Huerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流式知识编译（Streaming Knowledge Compilation）框架，通过材料性信号φ_t在文档流中预测重要性，在固定token预算下维护动态知识库，并在金融和维基百科两个领域验证了O(√T log K)的遗憾界，揭示了LLM作为评判者的后训练知识混杂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM维基系统将知识编译到预填充的KV缓存中，但假设语料库是静态的——这在信息环境演变时失效。需要一种能在文档流中动态维护编译知识库的方法，在未知未来查询的情况下最小化累积遗憾。

Method: 形式化流式知识编译问题；提出材料性信号φ_t(k,n)作为查询相关性的代理，实现主动固定重要性文档；在金融领域用冻结的Llama 3.1 8B分类头预测异常股票波动率作为φ_t（AUROC=0.728），在维基百科领域用异常编辑比率（AER）作为φ_t；证明O(√T log K)遗憾界。

Result: 金融领域：Llama 3.1分类头AUROC=0.728，预测材料性文章实现1.49倍更高的已实现远期波动率；累积遗憾收敛至-20.0（-0.12/步）。维基百科：累积遗憾收敛至+16.0（+0.13/步），正遗憾值证实维基编辑内容确实是后训练知识。QA评估发现LLM作为评判者在后训练知识上存在普遍混杂。

Conclusion: O(√T log K)遗憾保证适用于任何可通过流式信号预测知识差距的领域；遗憾分析（而非绝对QA分数）是编译知识系统可靠的评估指标；揭示了LLM-as-judge的后训练知识混杂问题。

Abstract: LLM wiki systems compile knowledge into pre-filled KV caches for efficient inference, but assume a static corpus -- an assumption that fails whenever the underlying information landscape evolves. We formalize Streaming Knowledge Compilation: given a document stream, a fixed token budget, and future queries unknown at ingestion time, maintain a compiled wiki that minimizes cumulative regret against an offline oracle with perfect foresight. The enabling insight is a materiality signal $φ_t(k,n)\in[0,1]$ that scores document importance for entity $k$ at time $t$, acting as a query-relevance surrogate for proactive pinning before queries arrive; we prove an $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ regret bound where $\varepsilon=\mathbb{E}[|φ_t-\hatφ_t|]$ is the only domain-specific quantity. We instantiate in two domains: finance, where $φ_t$ is abnormal stock volatility predicted by frozen Llama 3.1 8B classification head (AUROC = 0.728 on 76K articles, strict temporal split; $1.49\times$ higher realized forward volatility for predicted-material articles); and Wikipedia, where $φ_t$ is the Abnormal Edit Ratio (AER), a cross-sectionally normalized edit velocity -- showing the same algorithm generalizes beyond the finance domain. End-to-end QA evaluation on 173 matched pairs (finance) and 119 (Wikipedia) reveals a pervasive LLM-as-judge confound on post-training knowledge, establishing that regret analysis -- not absolute QA scores -- is the reliable evaluation metric for compiled knowledge systems. Finance cumulative regret converges to -20.0 (-0.12/step); Wikipedia to +16.0 (+0.13/step), with the positive sign confirming that Wikipedia edit content is genuinely post-training -- richer context consistently improves scores (No Wiki 3.80 vs. Oracle 4.74) -- and eliminates this confound. The $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ guarantee applies to any domain where knowledge gaps can be predicted from streaming signals.

</details>


### [37] [NOVA: Symbolic Regression Discovery of Interpretable Car-Following and Lane-Change Models with Driver Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10583)
*Ishak Abassi,Nassim Ali Bouazzouni,Farah Ibelaiden,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOVA是一个基于Rust的自主符号回归框架，能从原始轨迹数据中自动发现可解释的跟车与换道结构，在NGSIM数据集上超越现有基线，并以最小先验知识识别出人类驾驶行为的核心非线性项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶行为模型多依赖强先验假设或黑箱方法，缺乏从数据中自动发现可解释符号结构的能力，且跨场景泛化性差。

Method: 采用确定性Rust引擎搜索超10,000个候选代数结构，使用前向移位滚动均值作为预测目标，结合两种互补预处理流程，并在换道建模中扩展为多项Logit框架。

Result: 意图预测RMSE=1.376 m/s²（R²=15.57%），超越SR-LLM基线0.135 m/s²；换道建模在三类问题上达67.4%均衡准确率，超出基线+29.8个百分点；特征操作符零样本跨场景迁移R²损失<3个百分点。

Conclusion: NOVA能以最小行为先验自动发现可解释、可迁移的驾驶行为符号结构，发现与碰撞规避心理物理学理论一致的单一主导非线性项，为驾驶行为建模提供了新的自动化范式。

Abstract: We present NOVA, an autonomous symbolic regression framework that identifies interpretable car-following and lane-change structures from raw trajectory data with minimal behavioral priors. Applied to 4,765,788 active driving observations from the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 datasets, NOVA's deterministic Rust-powered search engine evaluates over 10,000 candidate algebraic structures and identifies a compact two-term acceleration model under a forward-shifted rolling-mean prediction target. Evaluated under two complementary preprocessing pipelines, NOVA achieves $RMSE = 1.376 m/s^2$ ($R^2 = 15.57\%$) on the intent-forecasting benchmark, outperforming the best recalibrated symbolic-regression baseline (SR-LLM, PNAS~2025) by 0.135 m/s$^2$ in RMSE under an identical evaluation protocol. Across eight independent experiments, a single dominant nonlinear term emerges as a robust backbone of human car-following; a residual-guided extension further links the selected structure to an established psychophysical theory of collision avoidance. The discovered feature operators transfer zero-shot between freeway sites with under 3 pp $R^2$ loss. Extended to lane-change modelling within a multinomial logit framework, NOVA achieves 67.4\% balanced accuracy under strict vehicle-ID holdout on 502 unseen drivers, surpassing existing lane-changing baselines by +29.8 percentage points on a three-class problem.

</details>


### [38] [Express Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10944)
*Albert Gong,Annabelle Michael Carrell,Raaz Dwivedi,Lester Mackey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Express的新工具，能将非因果注意力近似转换为具有相同保证的因果近似，与Thinformer结合后改进了最佳已知因果注意力保证，实现了$\log^{3/2}(n)/s$的近似误差，仅需$O(s)$内存和$O(s^2 \log^2(n))$压缩开销，并通过I/O感知的Triton实现相比FlashAttention 2取得了显著加速，在语言建模流水线的四个资源瓶颈（长上下文预填充、KV缓存压缩、长形式内存受限解码、长形式计算受限解码）中均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果注意力（causal attention）在语言建模中至关重要，但其近似方法在因果性保证上存在不足。现有方法缺乏将非因果注意力近似转换为因果注意力近似且保持相同保证的通用工具，导致长上下文场景下的资源瓶颈（内存、计算）难以突破。

Method: 提出Express工具，将非因果注意力近似转换为因果注意力近似并保留近似保证；与Thinformer结合实现改进的因果注意力近似；设计I/O感知的Triton实现以优化实际性能。

Result: 实现了$\log^{3/2}(n)/s$的近似误差，$O(s)$内存和$O(s^2 \log^2(n))$压缩开销；实际实现相比FlashAttention 2有显著加速；在长上下文预填充、KV缓存压缩、内存受限解码和计算受限解码四个场景中均克服了资源瓶颈。

Conclusion: Express提供了一种通用方法将非因果注意力近似转换为因果注意力近似，在理论上改进了因果注意力的近似保证，在实际中通过高效实现克服了语言建模中的多个资源瓶颈。

Abstract: We introduce a new tool, Express, for converting a non-causal attention approximation into a causal approximation with matching approximation guarantees. When combined with the state-of-the-art Thinformer approximation, Express improves upon the best known causal attention guarantees, delivering $\log^{3/2}(n)/s$ approximation error with only $O(s)$ memory and $O(s^2 \log^2(n))$ compression overhead for a sequence of length $n$. We pair these developments with an efficient I/O-aware Triton implementation, demonstrate substantial speedups over FlashAttention 2, and use Express to overcome four resource bottlenecks in the language modeling pipeline: long-context prefill, KV cache compression, long-form memory-constrained decoding, and long-form compute-constrained decoding.

</details>


### [39] [FailureScope: Cross-Regime Behavioral Diagnosis of Language Model Weaknesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09878)
*Nicholas Saban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FailureScope，一种基于行为诊断的方法，通过聚类评估探针的跨模型通过/失败模式（留一模法LOMO），在单轮基准、多轮对话和对抗性智能体攻击三种场景下生成稳定、可解释的失败分类体系。实验表明该方法在任务采样效率（Kendall's tau=0.81 vs 0.34）、跨模型失败预测（AUC 0.88）等方面显著优于基线，并揭示了一种元失败模式：LLM评判器ASR与实际执行之间存在73-100百分点的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准报告的是聚合准确率，但从业者需要知道模型具体缺乏哪些能力。现有方法无法提供细粒度、可移植的失败诊断工具，特别是跨不同评估场景（单轮、多轮、对抗性）的统一分析方法缺失。

Method: 提出FailureScope方法，核心思路是利用留一模法（LOMO）计算跨模型的通过/失败模式，然后对评估探针进行聚类，形成行为驱动的失败分类体系。在三个不同场景（单轮基准2664任务×18模型、多轮对话363任务、对抗性智能体攻击630轨迹）上验证。

Result: 在单轮任务中，基于分类体系的条件采样在50个任务时达到Kendall's tau=0.81（随机选择仅0.34），跨模型失败预测AUC达0.88。多轮和对抗性场景下聚类凝聚力保持强健。发现LLM评判器ASR与实际执行间存在73-100百分点的差距这一元失败模式。

Conclusion: 行为聚类是一种可移植的诊断原语，能够泛化到任何单一基准之外。FailureScope为模型评估提供了从聚合指标到细粒度能力诊断的桥梁，并释放了完整管线、三个标注语料库和跨场景分类体系。

Abstract: Standard benchmarks report aggregate accuracy, but practitioners need to know which specific capabilities a model lacks. We introduce FailureScope, a behavioral-diagnosis method that clusters evaluation probes by their cross-model pass/fail patterns (leave-one-model-out, LOMO), and show it yields stable, interpretable failure taxonomies across three regimes usually studied separately: single-turn benchmarks, multi-turn dialogue, and adversarial agent attacks. On 2,664 single-turn tasks across 18 models, taxonomy-conditioned sampling reaches Kendall's tau = 0.81 at 50 tasks (versus 0.34 for random selection), and cross-model failure prediction reaches AUC 0.88. The same primitive recovers interpretable clusters on a 363-task multi-turn corpus and on 630 adversarial agent traces, where it exposes a meta-failure mode: a 73-100 percentage-point gap between LLM-judge ASR and real execution. Cluster cohesion remains strong across all three regimes, which we take as evidence that behavioral clustering is a portable diagnosis primitive that generalizes beyond any single benchmark. We release the pipeline, three annotated corpora, and the cross-regime taxonomies.

</details>

*Tudor Pistol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化、显式条件化的可学习变换，将其表示为固定规范矩阵与数据自适应稀疏分量的乘积，在保留快速稳定分析变换优势的同时引入可控的自适应性。该算法基于不精确近端方法框架，利用新推导的闭式投影算子，在双稀疏变换学习问题上取得了最先进的结果，且计算成本显著低于密集变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析变换（如DFT、DCT）虽提供高效算法和鲁棒稀疏表示，但假定固定的数据先验，无法准确捕捉更受限信号类别的特定结构。数据自适应学习变换虽能减少变换域中的残差项，但需要在泛化倾向与最小逼近误差之间权衡。条件数被证明是该上下文中的良好度量指标。

Method: 将结构化、显式条件化的变换学习建模为固定规范矩阵（如DCT）与一个可学习的稀疏细化分量的乘积；在不精确近端方法框架下推导闭式投影算子，实现高效优化。

Result: 在双稀疏变换学习问题上达到最先进水平；与密集变体相比性能相当，但计算成本显著降低，有时收敛更快且更好地避免不良局部极小值。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化变换学习框架既能保持分析变换的快速稳定性，又能引入数据自适应性，是一种新颖且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Finding convenient spaces in which certain hypotheses regarding an assumed sparse structure of natural signals hold true has become a desirable result in recent research, its implications being reflected in areas such as data compression, noise reduction and feature extraction. While the extensively used analytical transforms, such as DFT or DCT, already provide efficient algorithms and robust sparse representations, they assume a fixed prior about the data, failing to accurately capture the specific structure of more restrictive classes of signals. To address this, the concept of a data-adaptive, learnt transform has been introduced in the literature, allowing for the reduction of a residual term in the transform domain. More recent studies have shown that the condition number serves as a good metric in this context, where the desired outcome alternates between a generalizing tendency and one that achieves minimal approximation error. Motivated by these considerations, we introduce the learning of a structured, explicitly conditioned transform formulated as the product of a fixed canonical matrix and a refining data-adaptive sparse component. This approach seeks to preserve the advantages of fast and stable analytical transforms, while introducing controllable adaptivity to the data. No references that concern this specific formulation have been identified so far, indicating its novelty. The proposed algorithm is motivated within the framework of inexact proximal methods, leveraging a newly derived closed-form projection operator. Empirical observations demonstrate state-of-the-art results on the doubly sparse transform learning problem and comparable performance with its dense variant at significantly lower computational costs and sometimes faster convergence and better avoidance of bad local minima.

</details>

Conclusion: 该序列核方法能够高效利用稀疏实验数据建模蛋白质属性景观，且结构信息整合进一步提升了多任务学习性能。

Abstract: Despite its importance to applications in protein design, predicting protein properties like binding affinity and thermostability from sparse experimental data remains a significant challenge. Accordingly, we introduce a class of sequence kernels that exploit evolutionary substitution matrices as well as local linearity and demonstrate that the resulting Gaussian processes provide data-efficient models of protein property landscapes, frequently outperforming alternatives that rely on foundation model embeddings. Furthermore--by learning what are in effect structure-aware substitution matrices--we show that our kernels can readily incorporate structural information from foundation models. We demonstrate that these structure-conditioned kernels are well suited to multi-task learning across multiple protein property landscapes and can decisively outperform local supervised learning methods.

</details>


### [42] [Operator Fusion for LLM Inference on the Tensix Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09879)
*Qingbo Wu,Ke Li,Wenzhu Wang,Jie Yu,Ruian Zhang,Lili Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer在Tenstorrent Tensix架构上的推理瓶颈，提出算子融合策略：将RMSNorm与矩阵乘法融合，在片上SRAM中连续执行内存密集型和计算密集型算子，减少DRAM读写；利用NoC多播机制支持多核并行，缓解DRAM带宽争用。在Wormhole平台测试Qwen2.5-0.5B等模型，注意力延迟降低37.44%，MLP降低15.89%，每解码层降低7.91%，PCC>98.75%。

Motivation: 解决Transformer模型在Tenstorrent Tensix架构上推理时，因算子间频繁的DRAM读写和调度开销导致的on-device推理瓶颈。

Method: 提出算子融合策略：将RMSNorm与自注意力和FFN中的矩阵乘法融合，使内存密集型和计算密集型算子在片上SRAM中连续执行；同时采用基于NoC的多播机制，通过行/列主节点在核心网格上高效分发输入和权重，支持多核并行。

Result: 在Wormhole平台上，Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-4B模型的注意力延迟降低最高37.44%，MLP延迟降低最高15.89%，每解码层延迟降低最高7.91%，Pearson相关系数保持在98.75%以上。

Conclusion: 所提算子融合策略在保证数值一致性的前提下，显著提升了端到端推理效率，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: This study addresses on-device inference bottlenecks of Transformer models on Tenstorrent's Tensix architecture and proposes an operator fusion strategy that enhances data locality. RMSNorm is fused with matrix multiplication in self-attention and in the FFN, enabling back-to-back execution of memory-bound and compute-bound operators in on-chip SRAM to significantly reduce DRAM reads/writes of intermediate results and scheduling overhead. To support multi-core parallelism, a NoC-based multicast mechanism is leveraged in which row/column master nodes efficiently distribute inputs and weights across the core mesh, alleviating DRAM bandwidth contention. Experiments on the Wormhole platform with Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen3-0.6B, and Qwen3-4B show up to 37.44% latency reduction for attention and 15.89% for MLP, with up to 7.91% reduction per decoder layer, while Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) remains above 98.75%, confirming significant end-to-end efficiency gains under numerical consistency.

</details>


### [43] [Data-Driven Dynamic Assortment in Online Platforms: Learning about Two Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11118)
*Rahul Roy,Nur Sunar,Jayashankar M. Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双边服务平台在信息不完全、顾客异质且离散时间环境下的动态动态组合优化问题。平台在不了解顾客和卖家双方选择模型参数的情况下，通过数据驱动算法学习参数并优化收益，实现了对数级增长的遗憾上界，并证明了该算法的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态组合优化研究通常仅考虑单边（顾客）选择参数未知的情况，而现实中的双边服务平台（如网约车、电商平台）中，顾客和卖家双方的选择行为均未知且相互影响，这一问题尚未被研究。

Method: 构建离散时间动态模型：每期顾客到达后平台展示卖家组合，顾客按MNL模型选择至多一个卖家发起交易；经过固定期数后，卖家按另一个MNL模型选择至多一个顾客接受交易。提出数据驱动算法同时学习双方MNL模型参数，并基于遗憾分析评估性能。

Result: 算法的在最坏情况下的遗憾随时间呈多对数（polylogarithmic）增长；推导了匹配的下界，证明了该算法的率最优性（rate optimality）。

Conclusion: 这是首个在双边选择参数均未知的动态组合优化问题，所提算法在理论上达到了最优的遗憾增长率，为双边平台在线学习与决策提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study a dynamic assortment problem on a two-sided service platform with incomplete information and heterogeneous customers in a discrete-time setting. In each period, a customer arrives seeking service, and the platform chooses an assortment of sellers to display. The customer then proposes a transaction to at most one seller in the assortment according to a multinomial logit choice model. After a fixed number of periods, sellers review the proposals they have received and each chooses at most one customer according to another multinomial logit choice model, after which the cycle repeats. A key challenge is that the platform does not know the choice-model parameters of either customers or sellers in advance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of a dynamic assortment problem in which both sides' choice parameters are unknown. We develop a data-driven algorithm that learns these parameters while optimizing the platform's objective over time. We evaluate performance using regret, which measures revenue loss relative to a clairvoyant benchmark that knows all parameters and customer arrivals in advance. We show that the algorithm's worst-case regret grows polylogarithmically over time, and we derive a matching lower bound, establishing its rate optimality.

</details>


### [44] [Limitations of Learning Tanh Neural Networks with Finite Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11104)
*Philipp Grohs,Matěj Trödler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限精度计算下，基于点评估学习tanh神经网络的局限性。通过迭代tanh激活函数构建尖锐局部化bump函数，证明了在有限精度设置下，任何基于m个样本的自适应随机算法在L^p范数下的收敛率无法超过蒙特卡洛率O(m^{-1/p})，除非采样预算随网络参数和架构大小指数增长。该结果揭示了有限精度对包含局部化bump函数的类别可学习性的根本限制，将先前ReLU网络的结果扩展到了tanh设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限精度计算对tanh神经网络学习能力的根本限制，特别是从点评估和L^p精度保证的角度，将ReLU网络的相关理论结果推广到tanh激活函数场景。

Method: 通过迭代tanh激活函数构建尖锐局部化的bump函数作为技术核心，结合有限精度计算假设，分析自适应随机算法的收敛率下界。

Result: 在有限精度设置下，任何基于m个样本的自适应随机算法的L^p收敛率无法突破蒙特卡洛率O(m^{-1/p})，除非采样预算随网络参数指数增长。

Conclusion: 有限精度计算对tanh神经网络的可学习性施加了根本性限制，bump函数的局部化特性是导致该下界的关键因素，该结论扩展了先前针对ReLU网络的理论结果。

Abstract: We investigate limitations of learning $\tanh$ neural networks from point evaluations under finite-precision computations and $L^p$ accuracy guarantees, building on Berner, Grohs, and Voigtländer (2023). Our approach is based on a novel construction of sharply localized bump functions via iterated $\tanh$ activations. Using this mechanism, we show that, in a finite-precision setting, no adaptive randomized algorithm based on $m$ samples can achieve a convergence rate higher than the Monte Carlo rate $O(m^{-1/p})$ in the $L^p$ norm, unless the sampling budget grows exponentially with the size of the network parameters and architecture. The results reveal fundamental limitations imposed by finite precision on the learnability of classes containing localized bump functions, extending previous results for ReLU networks to the $\tanh$ setting.

</details>


### [45] [Hyperparameter Learning for Latent Factorization of Tensors for Representation Learning to Large-scale Dynamic Weighted Directed Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09880)
*Yaqian Zhan,Jialan He,Tianzhu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于差分进化(DE)的自动超参数优化框架(DE-LFT)，用于求解大规模动态加权有向网络(DWDN)的张量隐式分解(LFT)模型，能够自动学习三个正则化参数，在四个真实数据集上取得了更低的MAE和RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LFT模型的性能严重依赖超参数选择，而手动调参或网格搜索需要大量计算资源和人力成本。

Method: 将差分进化(DE)算法集成到LFT模型的训练过程中，自动优化正则化参数λ₁、λ₂和λ₃，实现超参数空间的自适应搜索。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，DE-LFT相比手动调参的基线方法取得了更低的MAE和RMSE，同时减少了大量参数调优的需求。

Conclusion: DE-LFT能够有效自动搜索超参数空间，提升预测精度并降低调参成本。

Abstract: Large-scale dynamic weighted directed networks (DWDNs) are widely used to model time-varying interactions among nodes. Latent factorization of tensors (LFT) extracts target knowledge from DWDNs via low-rank embedding. However, similar to many machine learning models, the performance of LFT heavily depends on the selection of hyperparameters. In practice, these parameters are often tuned manually or through grid search, which requires significant computational resources and human effort. Motivated by this challenge, this paper proposes an automated hyperparameter optimization framework based on Differential Evolution (DE) for LFT (DE-LFT). The proposed method integrates DE into the training process of the LFT model to automatically learn optimal regularization parameters $λ_1$, $λ_2$ and $λ_3$. As a result, the model can adaptively search the hyperparameter space and improve prediction accuracy. Experimental results on four real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves lower MAE and RMSE compared with manually tuned baselines while reducing the need for extensive parameter tuning.

</details>


### [46] [Algorithmic and Minimax Complexities in Kernel Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11171)
*Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的MAIR框架将GP-UCB和DEC/MAMS方法置于共同的算法信息语言中，提出了结合两者优势的"受保护主算法"，并证明在过参数化模型中算法复杂度比类最小最大或DEC证书更具信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-UCB和DEC方法看似属于不同的理论体系，缺乏统一的视角来理解它们之间的关系和相对优势。

Method: 建立统一的MAIR框架，使用异质正半定算法先验，将GP-UCB分析（固定算法高斯过程先验，利用实现轨迹复杂度）和MAMS算法（优化类范围MAIR/DEC包络）进行泛化，并提出结合两者优点的 safeguarded master 算法。

Result: 算法信息与类最小最大系数回答不同问题，可导致不同差距；核Bandit为这种区别提供了数学上可见的清晰设定。

Conclusion: 在过参数化模型中，算法复杂度可以比类范围最小最大或DEC证书更具信息量，两者应被视为回答不同问题的互补工具。

Abstract: Gaussian-process upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) and decision-estimation-coefficient (DEC) methods may appear, at first sight, to belong to different theories. This paper places the two viewpoints in a common algorithmic-information language for frequentist RKHS bandits. GP-UCB fixes an algorithmic, rather than true, Gaussian-process prior and exploits realized-trajectory complexity together with computational tractability, whereas MAMS optimizes a robust class-wide MAIR/DEC envelope. Through the unified MAIR framework and heterogeneous positive-semidefinite algorithmic priors, we generalize both the GP-UCB analysis and the MAMS algorithm, propose a safeguarded master that combines their advantages, and provide a kernel-bandit construction showing that algorithmic complexity can be more informative than class-wide minimax or DEC certificates in overparameterized models. The resulting message is that algorithmic information and class-wide minimax coefficients answer different questions and can lead to different gaps; kernel bandits provide a clean setting in which this distinction becomes mathematically visible.

</details>


### [47] [Toward Calibrated, Fair, and accurate Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09881)
*Ryan Brown,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Face-Fairness (FF)框架，其核心贡献Face-Feature Tuning (FFT)是首个无需人口统计标签的深度伪造检测公平性方法，通过轻量级校准器基于冻结的人脸嵌入进行logit重映射，有效降低不同人口群体间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测器在不同人口群体间存在显著的性能差距，而现有公平性方法需要人口统计标签、重新训练或牺牲准确率，缺乏一种高效且无需标签的即插即用解决方案。

Method: 提出Face-Fairness框架，包含三个变体：FFT（核心方法，无需人口统计标签，基于冻结人脸嵌入进行logit重映射）、FF-Max（有标签时最大化最差组准确率）、FF-Discover（通过嵌入发现分组后最大化最差组准确率）。该方法为即插即用、检测器无关、无需身份属性。

Result: 在域内和跨数据集测试中，FF框架一致降低了FPR/TPR差距，提高了最小分组准确率，同时保持甚至提升了整体准确率，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: Face-Fairness框架为深度伪造检测提供了一种高效、通用、无需人口统计标签的偏差缓解方案，在保证性能的同时显著提升了公平性。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors show large performance gaps across demographic groups. Existing fairness approaches require demographic labels, retraining, or sacrifice accuracy. We introduce Face-Fairness (FF), a plug-and-play framework for bias mitigation. Our primary contribution, Face-Feature Tuning (FFT), is the first demographic label-free fairness method demonstrated for deepfake detection: a lightweight calibrator that performs a logit remapping conditioned on frozen face embeddings. We complement FFT with two variants: FF-Max, which maximizes worst-group accuracy when demographics are available, and FF-Discover, which does the same with embedding-discovered groups. Across in-domain and cross-dataset test settings, FF consistently reduces FPR/TPR gaps and improves minimum group accuracy while maintaining (often improving) overall accuracy. The approach is detector-agnostic, adds negligible runtime overhead, and requires no access to identity attributes.

</details>


### [48] [TD-Grokking: Learning from Zero-Reward Problems by Training-Time Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09883)
*Ningyuan Xi,Hao Xu,Hongsheng Xin,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中零奖励难题（所有采样轨迹均失败，无法提供优化信号），提出TD-Grokking框架，通过在训练时将难以解决的根问题递归分解为自包含、可验证的子问题，形成层次树结构，使可求解的叶节点提供非零奖励，从而将零奖励样本转化为可用训练信号。在数学和医疗任务上，该方法优于GRPO及所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法在零奖励问题（所有推理轨迹均失败）上无法提供优化信号，而过程监督、部分奖励分配等先验方法受任务限制或未能使策略模型获得解决原始难题的能力。

Method: 提出TD-Grokking，一种训练时分解框架：递归地将不可解的根问题分解为自包含、可验证的子问题，构建层次树结构，其中可解的叶节点提供非零奖励，从而为强化学习提供有效训练信号。

Result: 在数学和医疗任务上，TD-Grokking优于vanilla GRPO及所有基线方法，证明训练时分解能有效将零奖励样本转化为可用训练信号，实现一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 训练时分解（TD-Grokking）能有效解决零奖励问题，通过层次化子问题分解将零奖励转化为非零奖励，为强化学习在零奖励场景下的应用提供新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in reasoning tasks, largely driven by post-training paradigms, especially reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, a critical bottleneck persists: RLVR fails on highly challenging zero-reward problems, where all sampled reasoning trajectories yield uniformly failed outcomes, providing no optimization signal to drive model improvement. Prior efforts to address this limitation, such as dense process supervision, partial reward assignment, or prefix-guided exploration, suffer from inherent task constraints or do not fully equip the policy model with the capabilities necessary to solve the original intractable problems. To address this, we propose TD-Grokking, a training-time decomposition framework for zero-reward problems. It recursively decomposes intractable root problems into self-contained, verifiable subproblems, forming hierarchical trees where solvable leaves provide non-zero rewards. Evaluations on mathematical and medical tasks show that TD-Grokking outperforms vanilla GRPO as well as all baseline approaches. Together with detailed analysis, these results confirm that training-time decomposition effectively converts zero-reward examples into usable training signals, enabling consistent performance gains. Our code and datasets are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TD-Grokking-6567/.

</details>


### [49] [SHAPE: Coalition-Aware Expert Pruning for Sparse Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09886)
*Yuhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHAPE框架，利用夏普利值归因模拟专家协作，实现任务驱动的MoE模型专家剪枝，在保持精度的同时减少显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家剪枝方法独立评估专家重要性，忽略了MoE推理中专家通过路由协作产生输出的联合博弈特性，导致剪枝次优。

Method: 将路由轨迹建模为经验合作博弈，基于夏普利值对观察到的top-k专家联盟进行交互感知的归因；提出质量-覆盖率选择规则，在全局剪枝预算下保留每层中覆盖α比例非负夏普利质量的最小专家子集，并用二分法匹配目标保留率。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B、GPT-OSS-20B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite三个现代MoE骨干上，SHAPE在20%和40%剪枝率下保持竞争精度，显著降低峰值GPU显存，优于全局和逐层剪枝变体。

Conclusion: 基于夏普利值的合作博弈视角能有效识别MoE中高协作效用的关键专家，实现无需额外训练的高效剪枝。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models achieve strong quality with low per-token compute, yet their deployment is often limited by the memory wall: the full expert pool must remain resident to support token-dependent routing. Expert pruning is a direct remedy, but prior criteria typically score experts independently and overlook that MoE inference is inherently \emph{coalitional}, where outputs arise from routed top-$k$ expert combinations. We propose \textbf{SHAPE}, a task-driven pruning framework that explicitly models \emph{intra-layer} expert cooperation. SHAPE formulates routing traces on a small calibration set as an empirical cooperative game and assigns interaction-aware expert values via a Shapley-style attribution over observed top-$k$ coalitions, enabling the identification of experts that are essential for high-utility collaborations rather than merely frequent. To preserve MoE topology under a global pruning budget, SHAPE further introduces a \emph{quality-coverage} selection rule that retains, in each layer, the minimal expert subset covering an $α$ fraction of non-negative Shapley mass, while using bisection to match a target keep rate. Experiments on three modern MoE backbones (Qwen3-30B-A3B, GPT-OSS-20B, and DeepSeek-V2-Lite) across diverse benchmarks show that SHAPE consistently improves robustness over global and layer-wise pruning variants, maintaining competitive accuracy under 20\% and 40\% expert pruning without additional training and delivering clear reductions in peak GPU memory footprint. The open-source code is available at https://github.com/Alizen-1009/Shapley-Moe.

</details>


### [50] [SocraticPO: Policy Optimization via Interactive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09887)
*Zirui Liu,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Xianquan Wang,Jiayu Liu,Tingyue Pan,Qingchuan Li,Jing Sha,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SocraticPO框架，在强化学习推理中引入苏格拉底式自然语言教师指导，并配合奖励衰减机制，在科学推理基准上超越现有RL和自蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL使用标量结果奖励（如二元正确性）为优化提供方向，但很少解释模型应如何修正错误推理，这会鼓励捷径学习和脆弱策略。

Method: 在RL rollout过程中，学生先独立作答；若答案错误，教师诊断并提供简洁纠正性指导，学生随后在扩展上下文下继续推理。同时，教师干预后获得的正确答案仅获得衰减奖励，防止策略将教师帮助视为免费奖励路径。该方法仅修改rollout过程，保持标准期望奖励目标不变，可插拔到现有策略梯度后端（如Reinforce++），且教师仅提供文本级指导，可利用更强的黑盒教师模型。

Result: 在SciKnowEval本科级科学推理基准上，SocraticPO优于强RL和自蒸馏基线。消融实验表明，目标性指导和奖励衰减均必不可少，奖励衰减缓解了对辅助纠正的依赖。

Conclusion: SocraticPO通过自然语言教师指导与奖励衰减的联合设计，有效提升了LLM推理策略的学习质量，且具有即插即用的灵活性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models usually supervises reasoning with scalar outcome rewards, such as binary correctness. Such rewards provide an optimization direction but rarely explain how a model should revise its mistaken reasoning, which can encourage shortcut learning and brittle policies. We propose \textbf{SocraticPO} (Socratic Policy Optimization), a policy-optimization framework that augments RL rollouts with Socratic-style natural-language guidance. During rollout, the student first answers independently; if the answer is incorrect, a teacher diagnoses the attempt and provides concise corrective guidance, after which the student continues under the expanded context. Crucially, this guidance is paired with reward decay: correct answers obtained after teacher intervention only receive decayed rewards, preventing the policy from treating teacher help as a free path to reward. Since SocraticPO only modifies the rollout process while leaving the standard expected-reward objective intact, it can be plugged into existing policy-gradient backends such as Reinforce++. Moreover, because the teacher provides only text-level guidance, SocraticPO can leverage stronger black-box teacher models without requiring access to logits or distribution matching. On undergraduate-level scientific reasoning benchmarks from SciKnowEval, SocraticPO improves over strong RL and self-distillation baselines. Ablations show that both targeted guidance and reward decay are necessary, with reward decay mitigating reliance on assisted correction.

</details>


### [51] [SinkRec: Mitigating Semantic State Sink in Long Sequence Recommendation with Memory-Conditioned Gated Delta Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09888)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Zhipeng Wei,Ji Dai,Jie Chen,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SinkRec提出了一种混合记忆-循环过渡架构，通过残差向量量化解耦重复行为模式与动态转换建模，缓解线性注意力中的语义状态沉没问题，在保持线性时间效率的同时提升长序列推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力虽然避免了标准Transformer的二次计算成本，但其压缩的循环状态会被重复行为模式主导，导致语义状态沉没问题，即重复语义过度占据循环状态并偏置后续读取。

Method: 提出SinkRec混合记忆-循环过渡架构：1) 通过残差向量量化将重复局部模式外部化为可学习的条件记忆；2) 将检索到的编码重新注入，并将记忆键值对暴露给注意力模块；3) 引入TDGD，利用记忆纯化循环写入和读取，抑制被记忆覆盖的更新并移除与记忆对齐的读取响应。

Result: 在公开数据集和工业数据集上的实验证明了SinkRec的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: 该设计将重复语义从状态竞争信号转化为可记忆检索的模式，使循环状态专注于动态转换，在缓解语义状态沉没的同时保持线性时间效率。

Abstract: Linear attention provides an efficient backbone for long-sequence recommendation by avoiding the quadratic cost of standard Transformers, but its compressed recurrent state can be dominated by repetitive behavior patterns. We identify this phenomenon as semantic state sink, where recurring semantics over-occupy the recurrent state and bias subsequent readouts. To mitigate semantic state sink, we propose SinkRec, a hybrid memory-transition looped architecture that decouples collaborative behavioral pattern storage from dynamic transition modeling. SinkRec externalizes recurring local patterns into a learnable conditional memory through residual vector quantization, reinjects the retrieved codes, and exposes memory key-value pairs to the attention block. It further introduces Temporal-Aware State-Relation Differential Gated DeltaNet (TDGD), which uses memory to purify recurrent writing and reading by suppressing memory-covered updates and removing memory-aligned readout responses. This design turns recurring semantics from state-competing signals into memory-retrievable patterns, allowing the recurrent state to focus on dynamic transitions and alleviating semantic state sink with linear-time efficiency. Experiments on public and industrial datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of SinkRec.

</details>


### [52] [Optuna Constrained Tree-Structured Parzen Estimator Is a Joint Density Generalization of c-TPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09889)
*Shuhei Watanabe,Kaichi Irie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Optuna中广泛使用的约束TPE方法实际上是joint c-TPE，即使用联合似然的ECI采集函数，并证明了其对约束重复的不变性及与独立c-TPE的实践权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束超参数优化在实践中很常见，但Optuna广泛使用的约束TPE缺乏算法分析，需要澄清其理论基础并与独立c-TPE进行比较。

Method: 通过理论推导证明Optuna的约束TPE本质上是joint c-TPE，使用联合似然的ECI采集函数，并与独立c-TPE进行对比分析。

Result: 证明了joint c-TPE对约束重复具有不变性，而独立c-TPE会因重复约束的乘积累积而性能下降；同时指出了两种公式化之间的实践权衡。

Conclusion: 明确了Optuna约束TPE的理论基础，揭示了joint c-TPE与independent c-TPE的关键差异，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Constrained hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is common in practice, yet Optuna's widely used constrained TPE lacks algorithmic analysis. While c-TPE proposes an expected constrained improvement (ECI) approach assuming independence between the objective and constraints, Optuna uses a single joint density over both. We show that Optuna's constrained TPE is joint c-TPE -- the same ECI acquisition function using a joint likelihood. We demonstrate joint c-TPE is invariant to constraint duplication whereas independent c-TPE degrades as the product accumulates duplicated factors. We outline practical tradeoffs between the formulations and directions for future study.

</details>


### [53] [PreAct-Bench: Benchmarking Predictive Monitoring in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09890)
*Hainiu Xu,Italo Luis da Silva,Jiangnan Ye,Yuhao Wang,Wei Liu,Linyi Yang,Jonathan Richard Schwarz,Nicola Paoletti,Yulan He,Hanqi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出预测性监控（Predictive Monitoring）任务，即在部分行动轨迹上预测是否会导致不道德行为，并构建了PreActBench基准（1000条跨5个领域的有道德/不道德行动轨迹对）。实验表明，虽然人类表现良好，但现有模型在该任务上仍有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究仅能在完整轨迹后进行回顾性检测（即危害已发生后才识别），缺乏在行为执行前进行前瞻性风险推理的能力。

Method: 定义了Predictive Monitoring任务；构建PreActBench基准（1000条有道德/不道德轨迹对，跨5个领域）；提出Prefix Foresight F1评估指标；在部分轨迹上评估多种LLM、安全护栏模型和潜在探针方法。

Result: 人类在该任务上表现良好，但即使强模型在预测性监控上仍存在挑战，说明该任务具有难度。

Conclusion: LLM安全需要发展面向未来的风险推理能力，而不应仅依赖回顾性检测。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents capable of executing multi-step action trajectories toward a given objective. While existing safety research has focused on detecting unethical behavior from complete trajectories, this paradigm is fundamentally retrospective: it identifies harm only after it has already occurred. In this work, we study a critical yet overlooked safety task, which we term Predictive Monitoring: given only a partial action trajectory, can a model infer whether it will culminate in an unethical action before the overt action is executed? To support this task, we present PreActBench, a benchmark of 1,000 paired ethical and unethical action trajectories spanning five domains. We evaluate a range of LLMs, safety guardrail models, and latent probing methods across varying fractions of the action trajectory using our Prefix Foresight F1 metric. Results show that while humans achieve promising performance, predictive monitoring remains challenging even for strong models, highlighting the need for future-oriented risk reasoning in LLM safety.

</details>


### [54] [Representation Curriculum: Stagewise Training for Robust Ranking and Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09891)
*Ehsan Ebrahimzadeh,Sina Baharlouei,Abraham Bagherjeiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征课程（RC）方法，通过在训练过程中分阶段引入特征：先聚焦内容驱动的价值信号，再引入曝光依赖的信念信号并锚定内容路径，从而减少模型对历史信念信号的捷径依赖，提升冷启动和分布外泛化性能。在理论分析、公开基准和大规模电商搜索在线实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数字市场排名系统依赖曝光混杂信号（如流行度、CTR/CVR聚合、ID表征）具有强预测性，但这会导致学习捷径：模型过度依赖这些信号而忽视曝光独立的信号（如内容竞争力和语义亲和度），进而造成已存在物品固化、冷启动泛化差以及对分布偏移的鲁棒性降低。

Method: 提出表征课程（RC）训练干预方法，分阶段利用特征：前期聚焦内容驱动的价值信号，后期引入曝光依赖的信念信号，同时锚定内容路径使其接近已学到的价值表征，以抑制对历史信号的捷径依赖并缓解内容信号的梯度饥饿问题。RC不依赖于特定任务和假设类别，并提供了排序场景的具体实现。

Result: 在高斯线性岭回归条件下推导了闭式解和充分条件，证明RC在冷启动目标分布上严格降低总体风险，并给出与源域性能的Pareto权衡的量化关系。在公开的排序学习和推荐基准以及大规模电商搜索系统的随机在线实验中，RC显著将模型依赖从历史信念信号转向内容价值信号，在冷群体上获得一致提升，并在头部性能上保持可控的权衡。

Conclusion: RC能有效缓解排名系统中曝光混杂信号导致的学习捷径问题，提升冷启动泛化和分布外鲁棒性，且理论与实验支持其有效性。

Abstract: Ranking in digital marketplaces is a dynamic exposure-allocation mechanism: displayed items shape discovery trajectories and success events logged by the platform to update future allocation policies. Modern ranking systems rely heavily on exposure-confounded signals (e.g. popularity estimates, CTR/CVR aggregates, and ID-based representation), because they are highly predictive under stationary demand. Yet this predictive power can become a learning shortcut: early access to exposure-dependent belief signals steers optimization toward over-reliance on them and away from exposure-independent merit signals (e.g., content-based competitiveness and semantic affinity). Consequently, the learned policy tends to entrench incumbents and degrade cold-start generalization and robustness under distribution shift. We propose Representation Curriculum (RC), a training-time intervention that temporally stages feature utilization. RC foregrounds content-based merit signals initially, then introduces exposure-dependent belief signals while anchoring the content pathway near the learned merit representation, curbing shortcut reliance on historical signals and mitigating gradient starvation on content signals. We formalize RC independently of task and hypothesis class and provide ranking-specific instantiations. In a Gaussian linear ridge setting, we derive closed-form solutions and sufficient conditions under which RC strictly reduces population risk on a cold-start target distribution, with a quantified Pareto tradeoff against source performance. Experiments on public learning-to-rank and recommendation benchmarks, and randomized online experiments in a large-scale e-commerce search system, show that RC measurably shifts reliance from historical belief signals toward content-based merit signals and yields consistent gains on cold populations with a controlled trade-off in head performance.

</details>


### [55] [LMT: A Bayesian Framework for Causal Discovery from Textual Alarm Records in Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09892)
*Xiaofeng Xiao,Jianhong Chen,Qiuzhuang Sun,Naichen Shi,Xubo Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LMT框架，通过贝叶斯因果发现方法联合利用文本描述和时间戳信息，使用LLM提取语义因果信号构建先验分布，再结合泊松过程似然用时间戳证据修正，生成既可解释又有数据支持的因果图，在工程事件数据（尤其是小样本报警场景）中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程系统中的文本事件记录（如报警日志）中，工程师需要理解事件间的因果关系而非仅仅是相关性或重复模式。仅靠LLM从文本中提取的语义信号不足以进行准确的因果发现，因为语义模式可能混淆因果与相关或频繁序列模式。

Method: 提出LMT贝叶斯因果发现框架：1) 利用LLM从事件描述中提取语义因果信号，构建因果图先验分布；2) 通过基于泊松过程的似然函数纳入时间戳证据；3) 将LLM先验与时间戳统计证据相结合，得到因果图。

Result: 模拟研究表明LMT在不同设置下均有效，尤其是在小样本报警事件场景中具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 联合利用文本语义信息和时间戳统计证据的贝叶斯因果发现方法，能够生成既可解释又有数据支持的因果关系图，有效解决了仅依赖文本语义进行因果推断的不足。

Abstract: Textual event records, such as alarm logs, have become an increasingly common data source in engineering and manufacturing systems. Beyond identifying correlations or recurring patterns, engineers are often interested in understanding which types of events causally trigger or influence other events during system operation. Textual event descriptions may contain semantic clues about such causal relationships, and recent large language models (LLMs) provide a promising tool for extracting these signals. However, relying solely on LLM-encoded textual information is insufficient for accurate causal discovery, since semantic patterns do not directly reveal causal mechanisms and may confuse causation with correlation or frequent sequential patterns. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{LMT}, a Bayesian causal discovery framework for engineering event data that jointly leverages textual descriptions and timestamps. Specifically, LMT first uses LLMs to extract semantic causal signals from event descriptions and constructs a prior distribution over causal graphs among event types or event clusters. It then incorporates temporal evidence through a Poisson-process-based likelihood, allowing the LLM-informed prior to be refined by timestamp-based statistical evidence. By integrating the textual and temporal information, LMT produces a causal graph that is both interpretable and data-supported. Simulation studies show that the proposed framework is effective across different settings and is especially advantageous in small-sample alarm-event scenarios.

</details>


### [56] [A Navigable Manifold of Hypothesized Consciousness-Spectrum States in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09894)
*Sophie Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer嵌入空间中存在一个与人类意识光谱（从低层次反应性到高层次整合性）对齐的全局几何结构：相似状态的句子聚类形成局部连贯区域，低层和高层区域具有类凸稳定性，中间区域形成过渡走廊，且轨迹天然具有从低层向高层穿越的可导航性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型是否在表示空间中编码了类似于人类意识光谱的结构化、可解释的谱系，这对于模型引导、评估和对齐至关重要。

Method: 通过几何结构分析和动力学轨迹研究，在Transformer嵌入空间中探索与意识光谱对齐的全局组织几何结构，包括区域凸性分析和贪婪轨迹的可导航性验证。

Result: 嵌入空间展现出与意识光谱对齐的全局几何结构：高低层区域具有类凸稳定性，中间区域形成过渡走廊；无论是否使用效用引导，贪婪轨迹均一致地从低层区域穿越中间层到达高层区域。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间编码了与假设的意识光谱分类学对齐的结构化、可导航的几何结构，为分析和引导模型行为提供了表示层面的视角。

Abstract: Across contemplative, philosophical, and psychological accounts, human consciousness is often described along a similar spectrum, ranging from reactive and self-focused patterns to more integrative and coherent ones. Understanding whether language models encode such a structured, human-interpretable consciousness spectrum in representation space is important for model guidance, evaluation and alignment. In this work, we study the geometric structure and dynamics of patterns along this spectrum in transformer embedding spaces. We show that embeddings exhibit a globally organized geometry aligned with this spectrum: sentences associated with similar states cluster into locally coherent regions, forming a structured manifold. In particular, higher-level and lower-level regions exhibit convexity-like stability, while intermediate regions form a transition corridor. Dynamically, both utility-guided and geometry-only greedy trajectories consistently traverse from lower- to higher-level regions, passing through intermediate tiers, indicating that navigability is an intrinsic property of the representation space, guided but not dictated by a global directional signal. These results suggest that embedding spaces encode structured and navigable geometry aligned with a hypothesized consciousness-spectrum taxonomy, broadly inspired by recurring structural descriptions of human consciousness across contemplative traditions, philosophy, and modern psychology, providing a representation-level perspective for analyzing and guiding model behavior.

</details>


### [57] [TRAPS: Therapeutic Response Analysis via Pathway-informed Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09898)
*Sujoy Banik,Sayantan Chakraborty,Boishakhi Das Toma,Zainab Ghafoor,Ushashi Bhattacharjee,Koushik Howlader,Tirtho Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个统一的路径引导疗法反应建模基准，在相同条件下评估了BINN、GraphPath和PATH三种生物信息学架构在五个癌症队列（2622名患者）上的靶向分子治疗、放射治疗和生存预测三项临床任务。结果表明：无单一架构在所有任务中均最优，PATH在靶向分子治疗预测上最佳，BINN在生存预测上最可靠，GraphPath在前列腺癌靶向分子治疗预测中达到最高AUROC 0.92，而所有模型对放射治疗预测均无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的路径引导深度学习模型都是在孤立条件下开发和测试的，无法进行跨架构的公平比较；同时缺乏一个将路径结构化的深度学习作为联合治疗与生存预测问题的统一研究。

Method: 构建统一基准，使用Reactome通路活性评分编码来自TCGA的2622名患者数据，在相同数据和评估条件下联合训练BINN、GraphPath和PATH三种生物信息学架构，同时预测靶向分子治疗、放射治疗和六个月生存率三项临床结局。

Result: PATH在靶向分子治疗预测上整体最佳；BINN在生存预测上最可靠；GraphPath在前列腺癌靶向分子治疗预测上取得AUROC 0.92的最高分；所有模型对放射治疗预测均无效，因为其关键驱动因素为基因表达数据未捕捉的临床变量。

Conclusion: 无单一架构在所有任务中通用最佳，不同架构在不同任务上各具优势；放射治疗决策的关键驱动因素是临床变量而非基因表达数据；侧向共调控结构（GraphPath）在与窄靶向驱动程序的队列匹配时具有出色的判别能力，即使在类别极不平衡（仅11%阳性）的条件下也能达到优异性能。

Abstract: Cancer treatment planning requires decisions across multiple clinical dimensions at once. Clinicians must determine whether a patient should receive targeted molecular therapy, radiation therapy, and whether they are likely to survive beyond six months. Existing pathway-informed deep learning models have been developed and tested in isolation, making fair comparison across architectures impossible. We present the first unified benchmark for pathway-guided therapy response modeling, evaluating three biologically informed architectures, BINN, GraphPath, and PATH, across five cancer cohorts drawn from The Cancer Genome Atlas, representing 2,622 patients encoded using Reactome pathway activity scores. Each model is trained jointly on all three clinical outcomes under identical data and evaluation conditions, the first study to treat pathway-structured deep learning as a combined therapy and survival prediction problem. Our results show that no single architecture wins across all tasks: PATH performs best for targeted molecular therapy prediction overall, BINN is most reliable for survival prediction, and no model produces useful predictions for radiation therapy, as the key drivers of that decision are clinical variables not captured in gene expression data. Most strikingly, GraphPath achieves an AUROC of 0.92 on prostate targeted molecular therapy prediction, the highest score in the entire benchmark, demonstrating that lateral co-regulation structure produces exceptional discriminative power when matched to a cohort with a narrow targetable driver programme, even under conditions of extreme class imbalance at only 11\% positive prevalence.

</details>


### [58] [When Attribution Patching Lies: Diagnosis and a Second-Order Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09899)
*Luyang Zhang,Jialu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了归因修补（attribution patching）作为激活修补（activation patching）一阶近似时不可靠的根源，指出主要误差来自下游网络的非线性而非局部曲率。基于此提出三种实用工具：可靠性评分、误差界和HVP修正（仅需一次额外反向传播即可消除主导误差）。在五个模型家族（124M-9B参数）上的实验表明，HVP变体在精度上匹配或超越Integrated Gradients，同时计算成本显著更低，并支持Screen-Flag-Fix工作流以实现电路恢复的高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性需要识别语言模型中因果驱动行为的内部组件，但激活修补（gold-standard指标）计算成本过高，而实践中广泛使用的归因修补（一阶梯度近似）可靠性未知。本文旨在刻画归因修补不可靠的根本原因并给出实用解决方案。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示归因修补误差主要源于下游网络的非线性（而非局部曲率）；提出可靠性评分、误差界和Hessian-vector-product（HVP）修正（仅需一次额外反向传播消除主导误差）；在五个模型家族（124M-9B参数）上评估随机token和自然语言扰动场景。

Result: HVP是唯一在大规模模型上可行的二阶修正方法（Integrated Gradients计算成本过高）；多步HVP变体在精度上匹配或超越Integrated Gradients，且计算量显著更低；Screen-Flag-Fix工作流通过仅对不可靠组件投入计算资源提升了电路恢复保真度。

Conclusion: 归因修补的不可靠性可被有效量化和修正，HVP修正提供了一种计算高效的二阶校正方案，能够提升机械可解释性中电路识别的保真度。

Abstract: A central goal of mechanistic interpretability is to identify which internal components causally drive a language model's behavior. Because these importance estimates serve as the evidence for identifying circuits, systematic errors can lead to the misidentification of the underlying mechanisms. While activation patching provides a gold-standard causal metric, its computational cost is prohibitive at scale. Practitioners instead rely on attribution patching, a gradient-based, first-order approximation whose reliability remains poorly understood. In this work, we characterize the source of this unreliability, demonstrating that the dominant error stems from the non-linearities in the downstream network rather than local curvature at the patched component. This insight yields three practical tools: (i) a reliability score to detect untrustworthy estimates, (ii) error bounds quantifying potential attribution mis-specifications, and (iii) a Hessian-vector-product (HVP) correction that eliminates the leading-order error with only one additional backward pass. In evaluations across five model families (124M-9B parameters) and both random-token and naturalistic (name-swap) perturbations, HVP is the only second-order correction feasible at larger scale, where standard baselines like Integrated Gradients become computationally prohibitive. In comparative experiments, a multi-step HVP variant matches or exceeds the accuracy of Integrated Gradients at significantly lower compute, outperforming prior second-order baselines. These improvements lead to higher-fidelity circuit recovery on standard benchmarks and support a Screen-Flag-Fix workflow that targets computational effort only toward the components flagged as unreliable.

</details>


### [59] [LongMoE: Longitudinal Multimodal Learning via Trajectory-Aware Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09907)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Prakhar Kumar,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongMoE是一个统一框架，通过上下文感知插补、注意力令牌化、轨迹感知编码和稀疏MoE路由，同时解决多模态临床学习中的模态缺失和纵向动态两大挑战，在ADNI、OASIS-3和MIMIC-IV数据集上展示了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法孤立处理模态缺失（将每次就诊视为独立快照，忽略时间上下文）和纵向动态（假设模态完整，在缺失时性能下降）两大问题，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: LongMoE包含四个核心模块：1) 上下文感知插补模块处理模态缺失；2) 注意力令牌化模块捕获不规则就诊序列的频域时序模式；3) 轨迹感知编码器建模疾病进展；4) 上下文条件化稀疏MoE路由实现患者特定专家选择。

Result: 在ADNI、OASIS-3和MIMIC-IV数据集上，LongMoE在模态缺失或弱模态条件下提升了鲁棒性，同时在完整模态设置下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: LongMoE为纵向感知的多模态临床学习建立了坚实基础，有效统一了模态缺失和时序动态两大挑战的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical learning is increasingly important for integrating diverse patient data, including imaging, text, and personalised health records. However, it faces two fundamental challenges: i) modality missingness, where arbitrary subsets of modalities are unavailable at a given patient visit, ii) longitudinal dynamics, where the diagnostic significance of an observation depends on the patient's evolving disease trajectory over time. Existing methods address these challenges in isolation: missing-modality frameworks treat each visit as an independent static snapshot and discard temporal context, while longitudinal models often assume complete modality availability and degrade under systematic modality incompleteness. We propose LongMoE (Longitudinal Mixture-of-Experts), the unified framework to jointly address both challenges. LongMoE combines a context-aware imputation module with an attentional tokenization module that captures frequency-domain temporal patterns across irregular visit sequences, a trajectory-aware encoder for modeling disease progression, and context-conditioned Sparse MoE routing for patient-specific expert selection. Experiments on ADNI, OASIS-3, and MIMIC-IV show that LongMoE improves robustness under missing or weak contemporaneous modalities and remains competitive in full-modality settings, establishing a strong foundation for longitudinally-aware multimodal clinical learning.

</details>


### [60] [Mix, Don't Pick: Why Synthetic Corpus Composition Matters for Time Series Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09912)
*Aaryan Nagpal,Debdeep Sanyal,Murari Mandal,Dhruv Kumar,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 时间序列基础模型预训练中，合成数据生成器的选择至关重要但缺乏原则性方法，且生成器排名因架构而异。研究发现，简单的等权重混合所有生成器可匹配或超越最佳单个生成器，将混合数据与真实数据结合能获得最强预训练语料库。因此，合成预训练本质上是语料库组合问题，而非生成器选择问题，且组合选择应针对每个模型族验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型预训练中，合成生成器的选择缺乏原则性方法，且不同架构下生成器排名不稳定，导致选择不当会带来高达2倍的预测误差差距。

Method: 对11个生成器家族在Chronos-T5-Mini和Moirai-Small两种架构上进行评估，比较等权重混合策略与最佳单个生成器的效果，并进一步将混合数据与真实数据组合。

Result: 等权重混合所有生成器可匹配或超越最佳单个生成器；混合数据与真实数据组合能得到最强预训练语料库。

Conclusion: 合成预训练本质上是语料库组合问题，而非生成器选择问题，且组合选择应针对每个模型族验证而非假设可迁移。

Abstract: Choosing the wrong synthetic generator for time-series foundation model pretraining is costly: under identical training budgets, the best and worst generators produce up to a $2\times$ gap in forecasting error, yet the field has no principled way to make this choice. The problem is compounded by the fact that generator rankings are not stable across architectures: across 11 generator families evaluated on Chronos-T5-Mini and Moirai-Small trained from scratch, we find that which generators are useful depends on the model architecture. Rather than solving the generator selection problem, we sidestep it: a simple equal-weight mixture of all generators matches or beats the best individual generator for both architectures, and composing this mixture with real data yields the strongest pretraining corpora overall. Synthetic pretraining is therefore a corpus composition problem, not a generator selection problem, and composition choices should be validated per model family rather than assumed to transfer.

</details>


### [61] [IntentKV: Cross-Turn Intent-Aware KV Cache Pruning for Agent Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09916)
*Junjie Li,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IntentKV是一种面向多轮LLM Agent的KV缓存剪枝方法，通过跨轮次意图记忆和残差注意力头，在冻结基础LLM的前提下，大幅削减KV缓存峰值，几乎不损失精度。在8k KV预算下，Qwen3-8B和Qwen2.5-14B的平均峰值请求token分别下降23.9%和30.7%；在最长BCP查询上，峰值KV读操作减少92.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM Agent将短查询扩展为包含大量工具调用、搜索结果和中间推理的长轨迹，导致KV缓存（而非参数计算）成为主要服务瓶颈，因为KV内存和KV读取带宽单轨迹内增长数个数量级。

Method: IntentKV保持基础LLM冻结，维护会话级的跨轮次意图QueryMemory；利用记忆-注意力规则对实时历史token打分；添加零初始化的残差头，通过交叉注意力关注当前查询的K向量。为兼容前缀缓存，采用槽位映射重定向实现逐出：被丢弃的位置路由到哨兵死槽，保留的K/V行、RoPE相位和槽位身份保持不变。

Result: 在8k KV预算下，Qwen3-8B和Qwen2.5-14B的平均峰值请求token分别下降23.9%和30.7%；在Qwen2.5-14B上处理100个最长BCP查询时，最坏情况峰值请求token从92.3k降至20.5k（降77.8%），最坏情况原始KV读从411M降至31M（降92.6%）。

Conclusion: IntentKV在极紧的KV预算下几乎无精度损失地匹配了无剪枝全缓存基线，有效解决了长轨迹Agent中KV缓存主导服务瓶颈的问题。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM agents fan short queries into long trajectories of tool calls, search results, and intermediate reasoning. Both KV memory and KV read bandwidth grow by orders of magnitude across a single trajectory, making the key-value (KV) cache, not parameter compute, the dominant serving bottleneck for long-horizon agents. We introduce IntentKV, learned KV pruning that keeps the base LLM frozen. IntentKV maintains a session-level QueryMemory of cross-turn intent, scores live history tokens with a memory-attention rule, and adds a zero-initialized residual head with cross-attention over current-query K-vectors. To stay composable with prefix caches, eviction is a slot-map redirection: dropped positions route to a sentinel dead slot while surviving K/V rows, RoPE phases, and slot identities stay in place. IntentKV matches the no-pruning full-cache baseline with almost no accuracy drop under tight KV budgets: at an 8k KV budget, mean peak request tokens drop 23.9% on Qwen3-8B and 30.7% on Qwen2.5-14B. On the 100 longest BCP queries that all methods complete on Qwen2.5-14B, IntentKV-8k further cuts worst-case peak request tokens from 92.3k to 20.5k, a 77.8% reduction, and worst-case raw KV reads from 411M to 31M, a 92.6% reduction.

</details>


### [62] [SPDM: Geometry-Modulated State Space Modeling with Manifold Constraints for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09917)
*Xingsheng Chen,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPDM，一种将流形约束引入状态空间模型的多元时间序列预测架构，通过在对称正定流形上建模变量间相关性演化轨迹，利用黎曼几何特征引导SSM的选择性扫描，在保持Mamba线性时间复杂度的同时实现最先进的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间模型在处理多元时间序列时，通过扫描标记化的时间或空间序列，忽略了变量间不断演化的几何关联结构，缺乏对交叉变量相关性动态变化的显式建模。

Method: 提出SPDM架构，包含两个协作机制：(1) 流形轨迹路径，将SPD流形上动态演化的协方差矩阵投影到欧几里得切空间；(2) 几何门控方案，基于流形轨迹导出的几何信号直接调制SSM的内部选择性参数。通过黎曼几何特征、切空间线性和弗雷歇均值中心性作为几何正则化器。

Result: 在11个真实世界基准数据集上实现了最先进的预测性能，几何约束的状态空间动力学是性能提升的主导架构因素。

Conclusion: 将流形约束融入状态空间模型能够有效捕捉变量间的演化几何结构，在保持计算效率的同时提升预测精度，为多元时间序列预测提供了一种新的几何感知建模范式。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting requires capturing the continuously evolving correlation structure among interacting variables. Existing state-space models process time series by scanning tokenized temporal or spatial sequences, discarding the evolutionary geometric structure. We address this limitation by introducing manifold constraints into state-space modeling: treating the cross-variable correlation structure as a continuous trajectory on the symmetric positive definite manifold, whose Riemannian geometric features, tangent space linearity, and Frechet mean centrality act as a principled geometric regularizer that guides and stabilizes the selective scanning dynamics of SSMs. We propose SPDM, a geometry-aware SSM architecture that realizes this principle through two cooperating mechanisms: a manifold trajectory path that projects dynamically evolving covariance matrices from the SPD manifold to a Euclidean tangent space, and a geometric gating scheme that directly modulates SSM's internal selective parameters based on geometric signals derived from the manifold trajectory. The parameterization preserves the linear-time complexity of the Mamba parallel scan while embedding rich structural constraints, making the architecture preserve prediction accuracy and computational efficiency simultaneously. Extensive experiments on eleven real-world benchmark datasets establish state-of-the-art forecasting performance, and further studies confirm that geometrically constrained state-space dynamics are the dominant architectural factor behind its performance gains.

</details>


### [63] [Co-GLANCE: Uncertainty-Aware Active Perception for Heterogeneous Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09919)
*Michal P. Podolinsky,Neel P. Bhatt,Pranay Samineni,Rohan Siva,Christian Ellis,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Co-GLANCE是一个面向异构机器人团队的实时机载感知与决策系统，通过蒸馏视觉语言模型的语义推理能力，结合共形预测与选择性弃权机制，实现遮挡分割、机器人分配的不确定性量化与主动感知。在真实场景中，该系统相较基于云的视觉语言模型在遮挡分割精度上提升25%，机器人分配精度提升36%，推理延迟降低350倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人在非结构化户外环境中面临感知不确定性（如遮挡），单一视角无法保证可靠的场景理解。现有视觉语言模型虽然具备语义推理能力，但计算开销大、无法机载推理，且缺乏校准的不确定性量化。

Method: 将视觉语言模型的语义推理能力蒸馏为端到端模型，用于遮挡分割和机器人分配；结合共形预测与选择性弃权，为分割、分配和检测输出提供统计有效的覆盖保证；利用校准后的不确定性估计触发主动感知，调度最合适的机器人获取信息视角。

Result: 在真实场景中，Co-GLANCE在遮挡分割精度上比基于云的视觉语言模型基线提升25%，机器人分配精度提升36%，每帧推理延迟降低350倍。

Conclusion: Co-GLANCE在实现实时机载推理的同时，提供了具有统计保证的不确定性量化，有效解决了异构机器人团队在非结构化环境中的感知不确定性挑战。

Abstract: Perceptual uncertainty is a central challenge for heterogeneous robot teams operating in unstructured outdoor environments, where no single viewpoint affords reliable scene understanding. Perceptual uncertainty, arising from sources such as occlusions, manifests differently across robot viewpoints depending on scene structure. Detecting and resolving sources of perceptual uncertainty requires both scene-based contextual reasoning and capability-aware robot allocation. While vision-language models provide strong semantic priors for both, they are computationally prohibitive for onboard inference and lack calibrated uncertainty quantification. We introduce Co-GLANCE, a real-time onboard perception and decision-making system for uncertainty resolution in heterogeneous robot teams. Co-GLANCE distills the semantic reasoning capabilities of a vision-language model into an end-to-end model for occlusion segmentation and robot allocation, eliminating the need for cloud-based inference. To quantify perceptual uncertainty, Co-GLANCE combines conformal prediction with selective abstention to provide statistically valid coverage guarantees for segmentation, robot allocation, and detection outputs. These calibrated uncertainty estimates directly trigger active perception, dispatching the most appropriate robot to acquire informative viewpoints and resolve uncertainty. Across real-world scenarios, Co-GLANCE outperforms cloud-based vision-language model baselines in occlusion segmentation and robot allocation accuracy by 25% and 36%, respectively, while reducing per-frame inference latency 350x. We also release an air-ground dataset for future research. Code, videos, and dataset available at https://co-glance.github.io/ .

</details>


### [64] [Conformal Prediction for Neural Operators: Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification in Physics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09923)
*Michael Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将分裂共形预测应用于基于神经算子的物理仿真，提出了归一化共形预测方案，在稳态热传导基准上实现了89.1%的经验覆盖率（α=0.1），并提供了不确定性的分解框架（认知不确定性68%，偶然不确定性32%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在安全关键工程应用中缺乏具有正式覆盖保证的不确定性量化方法，而现有UQ方法（如MC Dropout和Deep Ensembles）只能提供相对不确定性估计，无法提供有限样本覆盖保证。

Method: 提出基于分裂共形预测的分布自由预测区间方法，并引入归一化共形预测方案，结合MC Dropout不确定性生成自适应宽度区间。

Result: 在稳态热传导基准上，33.7M参数、800训练样本、5个集成成员的设置下，目标水平α=0.1时达到89.1%的经验覆盖率，且预测区间具有空间自适应性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了具有正式覆盖保证的不确定性量化，并通过不确定性分解为数据收集和模型改进提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Neural operators such as the Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) have emerged as powerful surrogates for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), achieving speedups of several orders of magnitude over traditional numerical solvers. However, deploying these models in safety-critical engineering applications -- such as thermal management of electronic components and battery systems -- requires not only accurate point predictions but also rigorous uncertainty guarantees. Existing uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods for neural operators, including Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensembles, provide only relative uncertainty estimates without formal coverage guarantees. In this work, we propose the first application of split conformal prediction to neural operator-based physics simulation, providing distribution-free prediction intervals with finite-sample coverage guarantees. We further introduce a normalized conformal prediction scheme that leverages MC Dropout uncertainty to produce adaptive-width intervals, yielding tighter intervals in regions of low uncertainty and wider intervals where the model is less certain. Full-scale experiments (33.7M parameters, 800 training samples, 5 ensemble members, NVIDIA V100) on steady-state heat conduction benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves 89.1% empirical coverage at the target level of alpha=0.1, while producing spatially adaptive prediction intervals that reflect the underlying physical uncertainty structure.
