Overall, our formulation reveals a more fundamental design principle for SFT training and opens a broader search space for SFT objectives.

</details>


### [175] [A Systematic Approach for Selecting Trajectories for Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10938)
*Adam Nordling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种轨迹数据增强的选择策略（Outlierness、Diversity、Representativeness、Uncertainty、Random），发现系统性选择（尤其是Outlierness和Uncertainty）在稀疏数据集上能修复拓扑碎片、提升稳定性，但在高质量密集数据集上可能引入噪声，且高速度域存在物理局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹数据增强虽有几何扰动方法，但依赖随机选择，缺乏理解哪些轨迹应被增强以获得最大收益的系统框架。

Method: 构建系统化可扩展框架，评估五种选择策略（Outlierness、Diversity、Representativeness、Uncertainty、Random），在四个数据集（Foxes、Starkey、AIS、Car）上结合线性和非线性机器学习模型测试，并集成Optuna超参数优化。

Result: 系统性策略（尤其是Outlierness和Uncertainty）比随机基线更稳定，在稀疏数据集中可修复拓扑碎片，但在高质量密集数据集中会引入噪声；高速度域中存在物理发散限制。

Conclusion: 系统性选择并非通用解决方案，增强的有效性取决于数据集特性：稀疏数据受益，密集数据反受损。

Abstract: Trajectory data augmentation is a promising approach to mitigate data scarcity in machine learning applications, but its utility has been limited by the complexity of preserving spatio-temporal coherence. Although prior work demonstrated the viability of geometric perturbation, it relied on naive random selection, leaving a critical gap in understanding which trajectories should be augmented for maximal benefit. This thesis addresses this gap by developing a systematic and scalable framework to evaluate five systematic selection strategies: Outlierness, Diversity, Representativeness, Uncertainty, and Random selection. These strategies were rigorously tested across four datasets covering animal behavior (Foxes and Starkey), maritime traffic (AIS), and urban traffic (Car) using a suite of linear and non-linear machine learning models. As part of this evaluation, an Optuna-based hyperparameter optimization loop was integrated to empirically identify the best-performing augmentation parameters for each dataset within the explored search space. The results indicate that, while systematic selection is not a universal solution, it offers distinct advantages over the random baseline. Systematic strategies, particularly Outlierness and Uncertainty, demonstrated higher stability and were less prone to performance degradation observed with random sampling in dense datasets. However, the findings also reveal that the value of augmentation is strictly conditional. Visual analysis via UMAP demonstrates that while systematic augmentation successfully repairs topological fragmentation in sparse datasets, it can act as a corrupting noise signal in high-quality, dense datasets. Furthermore, the study identified physical limitations in high-velocity domains, where standard perturbation techniques lead to divergence in feature space...

</details>


### [176] [Population-Aware Physics-Informed Neural Particle Flow for Bayesian Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10959)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出群体感知物理信息神经粒子流（PA-PINPF），通过在粒子更新中加入置换不变的Deep Sets群体编码，使粒子流感知整体粒子分布。该方法的特征群体编码（PA-PINPF-Feature）利用完整的局部物理信息特征向量，同时捕捉粒子云几何和贝叶斯传输几何。实验表明群体感知变体优于标准PINPF，特征群体编码表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINPF的粒子速度模型独立处理每个粒子，未显式利用经验粒子群体的整体信息，导致传输决策缺乏全局上下文。

Method: 提出PA-PINPF，在粒子更新中引入置换不变的Deep Sets表示来编码完整粒子集。设计了两种编码器：PA-PINPF-State（对粒子状态汇总）和PA-PINPF-Feature（对局部物理信息特征向量汇总，包括粒子位置、伪时间、测量信息、似然值和分数信息）。保持无监督的物理信息残差目标，无需真实后验样本。

Result: 在距离测量任务和非线性到达时间差后验传输实验中，两种群体感知变体均优于逐粒子的PINPF，其中特征群体编码（PA-PINPF-Feature）表现最强。

Conclusion: 群体级别的物理特征能为学习的贝叶斯粒子传输提供有用的全局信息，特征级群体编码是更优的选择。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural particle flow (PINPF) learns a deterministic transport field that moves particles from a prior distribution toward a Bayesian posterior while enforcing the governing probability-evolution equation. However, the standard PINPF velocity model processes particles independently and therefore does not explicitly condition its transport decisions on the empirical particle population. This paper introduces population-aware PINPF (PA-PINPF), which augments each particle update with a permutation-invariant Deep Sets representation of the full particle set. We investigate two population encoders. PA-PINPF-State summarizes the particle states, whereas PA-PINPF-Feature summarizes the complete local physics-informed feature vectors, including particle position, pseudo-time, measurement information, likelihood values, and score information. The latter allows the population context to represent not only particle-cloud geometry, but also the population-level Bayesian transport geometry. The methods retain the original unsupervised physics-informed residual objective and require no ground-truth posterior samples during training. Experiments on range-measurement tasks and nonlinear time-difference-of-arrival posterior transport demonstrate that both population-aware variants improve over particle-wise PINPF, while feature-population encoding provides the strongest performance. These results show that population-level physics features provide useful global information for learned Bayesian particle transport.

</details>


### [177] [Data-Driven Runway and Taxiway Exits Prediction of Landing Aircraft: A Case Study at Hartsfield-Jackson Atlanta International Airport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11017)
*Alex Porcayo,Yutian Pang,Maria Thomas,John-Paul Clarke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段数据驱动决策辅助框架，用于预测亚特兰大机场（KATL）的到达航班滑入决策：第一阶段预测跑道出口选择，第二阶段预测是否穿越活动起飞跑道或使用绕行滑行道。XGBoost和LightGBM表现最佳，进近速度是出口选择的关键特征，而离港率、穿越率等是绕行决策的关键预测因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机场地面运行日益成为高吞吐量枢纽的性能瓶颈，现有研究缺乏对到达航班滑入决策（尤其是跑道出口选择和是否穿越活动跑道）的系统性预测方法，需要开发数据驱动的决策辅助工具来支持管制员态势感知。

Method: 两阶段数据驱动决策框架：Stage I预测到达航班选择的跑道出口；Stage II预测飞机在该出口后是否穿越活动起飞跑道或使用绕行滑行道。使用ASDE-X表面轨迹、飞机特征、停机位目的地、短期交通率和天气数据训练9种分类器（包括Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost等），并通过t-SNE和UMAP分析特征空间重叠。

Result: Stage I准确率0.86-0.89，macro-F1为0.40-0.50；Stage II准确率0.70-0.74，macro-F1为0.28-0.55。XGBoost和LightGBM优于Random Forest。进近速度是出口选择的主要驱动因素；离港率、穿越率、停机位目的地及所选出口是穿越与绕行决策的最强预测因子。少数类因特征空间重叠而预测困难。

Conclusion: 该框架通过可校准、可解释的预测支持管制员态势感知，同时将最终路由决策权保留给人类，表明数据驱动方法能有效辅助机场地面运行决策。

Abstract: Airport surface operations increasingly constrain performance at high-throughput hubs. This study examines arrival taxi-in decisions at Hartsfield-Jackson Atlanta International Airport (KATL) and proposes a two-stage, data-driven decision aid that mirrors controller workflow. Stage I predicts the runway exit selected by an arriving aircraft. Stage II predicts whether, given that exit, the aircraft will cross the active departure runway at a designated point or use the end-around taxiway. Models are trained using ASDE-X surface trajectories, aircraft characteristics, ramp destinations, short-horizon traffic rates, and weather across multiple look-back windows. We benchmark nine classifiers, including Random Forest, XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost, and evaluate accuracy, macro-F1, precision-recall behavior, confusion matrices, Brier score, and Expected Calibration Error. Across east and west flows, XGBoost and LightGBM outperform Random Forest. Stage I achieves 0.86-0.89 accuracy with macro-F1 scores of 0.40-0.50, while Stage II achieves 0.70-0.74 accuracy with macro-F1 scores of 0.28-0.55. Feature-importance analysis shows that approach speed is the main driver of exit choice. Departure rate, crossing rate, ramp destination, and, for west flow, the selected exit are the strongest predictors of crossing versus end-around routing. Minority classes remain harder to predict because of feature-space overlap, as shown by t-SNE and UMAP analyses. The proposed framework supports controller situational awareness through calibrated, explainable predictions while preserving human responsibility for final routing decisions.

</details>


### [178] [Flow-DPPO: Divergence Proximal Policy Optimization for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11025)
*Bowen Ping,Xiangxin Zhou,Penghui Qi,Minnan Luo,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow-DPPO方法，用精确可计算的KL散度约束替代PPO中的比率裁剪，解决了在线强化学习微调流匹配模型时比率裁剪不适用的问题，在图像和视频生成任务上取得了更好的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线RL方法（如Flow-GRPO、CPS）将去噪过程建模为MDP并应用PPO风格的比率裁剪来约束信任区域。但比率裁剪对流模型结构上不适用：新旧策略的概率比率是有噪的单样本估计，会导致轨迹某些区域过度约束而另一些区域约束不足。

Method: 提出Flow-DPPO，用散度近端约束替代比率裁剪。关键洞察是流模型中每步策略是高斯分布，使得新旧策略之间的KL散度可以精确且低成本地计算。采用非对称散度掩码，仅当更新同时偏离信任区域且违反散度阈值时才阻止梯度更新。

Result: 实验表明Flow-DPPO实现了更高的奖励和更好的KL近端效率，缓解了灾难性遗忘，促进了多目标平衡优化，并在比率裁剪失效的多轮训练中保持稳定。

Conclusion: Flow-DPPO通过精确KL散度约束替代比率裁剪，有效解决了流模型在线RL微调中的信任区域约束问题，为流匹配模型的强化学习训练提供了更稳定高效的方法。

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated that online reinforcement learning (RL) can substantially improve the quality and alignment of flow matching models for image and video generation. Methods such as Flow-GRPO and CPS cast the denoising process as a Markov Decision Process and apply PPO-style ratio clipping to enforce a trust region. However, we argue that ratio clipping is structurally ill-suited for flow models: the probability ratio between new and old policies is a noisy, single-sample estimate of the true policy divergence, leading to over-constraining in some regions of the trajectory and under-constraining in others. We propose Flow-DPPO (Flow Divergence Proximal Policy Optimization), which replaces ratio clipping with a divergence proximal constraint. A key observation is that the per-step policy in flow models is Gaussian, enabling exact and cheap computation of the KL divergence between old and new policies. Flow-DPPO employs an asymmetric divergence mask that blocks gradient updates only when they simultaneously move away from the trusted region and violate the divergence threshold. Experiments show that Flow-DPPO achieves higher rewards with better KL-proximal efficiency, alleviates catastrophic forgetting, promotes balanced multi-objective optimization, and enables stable multi-epoch training where ratio clipping degrades. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Tencent-Hunyuan/UniRL/tree/main/FlowDPPO.

</details>


### [179] [GRAFT: Gain-Recalibrated Adapters for Transformer-Based Neural Population Activity Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11066)
*Xiangsheng Ge,Yang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAFT，一种基于Transformer的神经群体活动模型，通过分离可复用的时间动态与可重新校准的神经元接口，在NLB'21 MC Maze数据集上达到0.3866 co-bps的新SOTA，并在跨天协议中仅更新9.21%参数即可实现高效重校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经群体活动模型的读写层与固定神经元集绑定，导致在长期脑机接口中无法适应神经元身份、数量及响应统计的跨天变化，限制了模型的复用性。

Method: 提出GRAFT模型，包含一个共享的Transformer时间动态骨干网络和一个可重新校准的神经元接口模块。神经元接口控制记录神经元的进入与退出，辅助增益和位置机制支持Transformer内部的神经活动建模。

Result: 在NLB'21 MC Maze标准协议下集成模型达到0.3866 co-bps，刷新SOTA；在跨天协议中，仅更新9.21%参数即可在Large/Medium/Small数据集上分别达到0.3749、0.3112和0.3152 co-bps。

Conclusion: 接口-骨干分离策略既能支持基于Transformer的强大神经群体活动建模，又能实现数据高效的跨天重校准，表明该范式在长期脑机接口中具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Neural population activity models can recover rich temporal structure from binned spikes, but their read-in and readout layers often remain tied to a fixed set of recorded neurons. This coupling limits reuse in long-term brain-computer interfaces, where recorded neuron identities, counts, and response statistics can change across days. We introduce GRAFT, a Transformer-based neural population activity model that separates reusable temporal dynamics from a recalibratable neuron interface. The neuron interface controls how recorded neurons enter and leave the shared backbone, and auxiliary gain and positional mechanisms support neural activity modeling inside the Transformer. On MC Maze under the standard NLB'21 protocol, GRAFT reaches 0.3866 co-bps as an ensemble, setting a new state of the art on the primary co-bps metric among public and reported NLB'21 results. In a cross-day protocol constructed from the NLB'21 MC Maze dataset series, GRAFT recalibrates from MC Maze to the scaled MC Maze datasets (Large/Medium/Small) by updating only 9.21% of parameters, reaching 0.3749, 0.3112, and 0.3152 co-bps with restricted target-day support sets. These results show that the same interface-backbone separation supports both strong Transformer-based neural population activity modeling and data-efficient cross-day recalibration.

</details>


### [180] [Exploring the Design Space of Reward Backpropagation for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11075)
*Ruoyu Wang,Boye Niu,Xiangxin Zhou,Yushi Huang,Tongliang Liu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowBP提出了一种统一代理轨迹框架用于文本到图像流匹配模型的对齐，通过将反向传播过程本身作为设计对象，解决了直接奖励反向传播中的激活存储和梯度膨胀问题，在多个模型和指标上优于直接梯度基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接奖励反向传播虽样本高效，但存在两大病理问题：现代模型规模下无法沿完整采样轨迹存储激活值，以及跨步骤的链式雅可比乘积使奖励梯度在传播到早期索引时膨胀。Connector类方法（如LeapAlign）通过短固定路径替代完整反向轨迹，揭示了采样与优化的解耦，但其梯度质量取决于短路径对完整rollout的近似精度。

Method: FlowBP将反向轨迹本身作为设计对象，构建无梯度的缓存rollout用于采样，然后从缓存和选择性重新前向的velocity中构建轻量级反向代理。该框架分离了四个设计选择：奖励模型输入、激活集、积分权重和桥接耦合。基于此框架实例化了三个变体：FlowBP-Sparse（稀疏欧拉重建）、FlowBP-Bridge（受控桥接耦合）、FlowBP-Lagrange（高阶跳跃求积）。三者均通过激活集大小约束内存，并将梯度链限制至多一个雅可比因子。

Result: 在SD3.5-M、FLUX.1-dev和FLUX.2-Klein-base三个模型上，以偏好、质量和组合指标进行评估，三个变体在大多数指标上优于直接梯度基线方法。

Conclusion: FlowBP通过统一的代理轨迹框架有效解决了直接反向传播的存储和梯度膨胀问题，同时恢复了先前直接梯度方法作为特例，为流匹配模型的人类偏好对齐提供了更高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Aligning text-to-image flow matching models with human preferences via direct reward backpropagation is sample-efficient but hampered by two well-known pathologies: activations cannot be stored across the full sampling trajectory at modern model scale, and chained Jacobian products across steps inflate the reward gradient as it travels back to early indices. Connector-based methods, such as LeapAlign, address these issues by replacing the full backward trajectory with a short pinned path, highlighting a useful decoupling between sampling and optimization. However, the quality of the resulting gradient depends on how accurately this short path approximates the full rollout, especially over long intervals. We propose FlowBP, a unified surrogate-trajectory framework that treats the backward trajectory itself as the design object. FlowBP keeps a no-gradient cached rollout for sampling, then builds a lightweight backward surrogate from cached and selectively re-forwarded velocities. This view separates four choices: the reward-model input, active set, integration weights, and bridge coupling, and recovers prior direct-gradient methods as particular settings. Within this framework, we instantiate three variants: FlowBP-Sparse uses sparse Euler reconstruction, FlowBP-Bridge adds controlled bridge coupling, and FlowBP-Lagrange raises the order of leap quadrature. All three bound memory by the active-set size and limit gradient chaining to at most one Jacobian factor. Across SD3.5-M, FLUX.1-dev, and FLUX.2-Klein-base on preference, quality, and compositional metrics, the three variants improve over direct-gradient baselines on most metrics.

</details>


### [181] [Overcoming Rank Collapse in Feedback Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11123)
*Gauthier Boeshertz,Razvan Pascanu,Claudia Clopath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现反馈对齐（FA）在深层架构中效果不佳的关键原因在于误差信号的有效秩过低，限制了参数空间的探索。通过引入Muon优化器和隐藏活动归一化来提升信号的有效维度，在CIFAR100上获得了9个百分点的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播（BP）因要求反馈权重为前向权重的转置而被认为生物不 plausible，FA通过固定随机反馈权重解决此问题，但无法有效扩展到深层架构，其根本原因尚不明确。

Method: 1) 比较BP与FA模型在CIFAR10上的有效秩差异；2) 提出两种提升FA有效维度的方法：Muon优化器（正交化权重更新）和隐藏活动归一化（促进激活正交性）。

Result: FA误差信号的有效秩显著低于BP；所提方法在更大架构和基准上持续优于FA基线，如CIFAR100上ResNet-18准确率提升9个百分点。

Conclusion: 低维梯度动力学是阻碍FA扩展的关键障碍，诱导更高维度的更新几何是扩展BP替代方案的有前景方向。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) is widely viewed as biologically implausible, in part because it requires feedback weights to be the transpose of forward weights for error propagation. Interestingly, when training a network with fixed random feedback weights to circumvent this issue, learning aligns the forward weights with the feedback weights, leading the backpropagated error signal to become an approximation of the standard gradient used by BP. This process, called Feedback Alignment (FA), occurs in MLPs and very shallow CNNs but does not scale well to deeper architectures. In this work, we first investigated differences between BP and FA models, trained on CIFAR10, specifically focusing on the effective rank of the signal. We found that the FA error has a considerably lower rank and hence is constrained to a lower-dimensional subspace compared to BP, limiting exploration of the parameter space. Motivated by this observation, we evaluated two mechanisms for increasing the effective dimensionality of FA: Muon, an optimiser that orthogonalises weight updates; and hidden activity normalisation, which promotes activation orthogonality. Across larger architectures and benchmarks, we find that these methods consistently improve over FA baselines, for example, on CIFAR100 with a Resnet-18, accuracy increases by 9 percentage points. Our results identify low-dimensional gradient dynamics as a key obstacle to scaling FA and suggest that inducing higher-dimensional update geometry is a promising route toward scaling alternatives to backpropagation.

</details>


### [182] [Robust Regression of General ReLUs with Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11130)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯分布下一般ReLU函数的对抗性学习问题，提出了首个计算高效的交互式学习算法，仅需$d polylog(1/ε)+\tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$个黑盒标签查询即可达到$O(opt)+ε$误差，并证明了该查询复杂度在定性上近乎最优，同时揭示了查询访问对于改进被动学习标签复杂度的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在被动学习场景下已有高效算法，但交互式学习（如主动学习）能否利用查询访问来显著降低标签复杂度尚不清楚。本文旨在探索交互式设置下学习一般ReLU的标签效率，并回答查询访问是否本质上优于被动学习。

Method: 提出首个计算高效的交互式学习算法，使用黑盒标签查询（即查询未标注样本的标签），结合$d polylog(1/ε)+\tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$个查询，其中$p$为目标函数的偏置。算法针对高斯分布下的一般ReLU（非齐次）进行对抗性学习，优化平方损失。

Result: 算法以$O(opt)+ε$的误差学习一般ReLU，查询复杂度在定性上近乎最优（忽略计算约束）。此外，证明了基于池的主动学习至少需要$\tildeΩ(d/ε)$个标签，除非抽取超多项式数量的未标注样本，表明查询访问对提升被动学习效率是必要的。

Conclusion: 交互式设置可以显著降低学习一般ReLU的标签复杂度（从$poly(d,1/ε)$降至$d polylog(1/ε)+\tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$），且该查询界在理论上接近下界；同时，查询访问是突破被动学习标签复杂度的关键。

Abstract: We study the task of agnostically learning general (as opposed to homogeneous) ReLUs under the Gaussian distribution with respect to the squared loss. In the passive learning setting, recent work gave a computationally efficient algorithm that uses $poly(d,1/ε)$ labeled examples and outputs a hypothesis with error $O(opt)+ε$, where $opt$ is the squared loss of the best fit ReLU. Here we focus on the interactive setting, where the learner has some form of query access to the labels of unlabeled examples. Our main result is the first computationally efficient learner that uses $d polylog(1/ε)+\tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$ black-box label queries, where $p$ is the bias of the target function, and achieves error $O(opt)+ε$. We complement our algorithmic result by showing that its query complexity bound is qualitatively near-optimal, even ignoring computational constraints. Finally, we establish that query access is essentially necessary to improve on the label complexity of passive learning. Specifically, for pool-based active learning, any active learner requires $\tildeΩ(d/ε)$ labels, unless it draws a super-polynomial number of unlabeled examples.

</details>


### [183] [First-Order Trajectory Matching: Fast Ensemble Predictions of Chaotic, Turbulent, Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11138)
*Shreya Jha,Timo Schorlepp,Nicholas Geissler,Jules Berman,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一阶轨迹匹配（FTM）方法，通过学习随机系统轨迹的一阶局部概率质量传输，直接获得概率流速度（current velocity），无需估计漂移、扩散或得分函数。FTM能够保持时间边际分布并捕捉通量、环流、穿越势垒流等轨迹量。稳定性分析表明，当时间分辨率和样本量适当平衡时，单步无仿真FTM损失是稳定的。实验证明FTM在低确定性 rollout 成本下提供轨迹感知的系综预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在建模随机系统轨迹时，通常需要估计漂移、扩散或得分函数，计算成本高且难以直接捕捉通量、环流等轨迹层面的物理量。FTM旨在通过直接学习概率流速度来避免这些中间估计，同时保持时间边际分布并捕获轨迹相关的流特征。

Method: FTM通过匹配轨迹的一阶对称运动来学习概率流速度（current velocity）。该方法直接从未配对的轨迹数据中学习，无需仿真或迭代求解。核心是建立一个单步、无仿真的损失函数，通过平衡时间分辨率和样本量来保证稳定性。

Result: 在多个随机动力系统和PDE示例上，FTM以低确定性rollout成本实现了轨迹感知的系综预测，能够准确捕捉通量、环流和穿越势垒流等概率电流量。

Conclusion: FTM提供了一种高效、稳定的替代方案，用于直接从轨迹数据学习随机系统的概率流，在保持边际分布的同时捕获轨迹层面的动态特征，具有较好的理论稳定性和实证性能。

Abstract: We introduce First-Order Trajectory Matching (FTM), a surrogate-modeling method that learns the first-order local transport of probability mass from trajectories of stochastic systems. By matching the symmetric first-order motion of trajectories, FTM learns the probability current velocity, whose flow preserves time marginals to match ensemble averages, while also capturing current-like trajectory quantities such as fluxes, circulations, and barrier-crossing currents. FTM learns the current velocity directly from trajectories, avoiding drift, diffusion, and score estimation. Our stability analysis separates discretization error from sampling variance and shows that the one-step simulation-free FTM loss is stable when temporal resolution and sample size are properly balanced. Across stochastic dynamical systems and PDE examples, we empirically demonstrate that FTM provides trajectory-aware ensemble predictions at low, deterministic-rollout cost.

</details>


### [184] [OncoTraj: a public benchmark for longitudinal resistance prediction in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer on osimertinib](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11144)
*Abhijoy Sarkar,Aarchi Singh Thakur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OncoTraj是一个针对EGFR突变非小细胞肺癌患者接受奥希替尼一线治疗后耐药演化的公开基准数据集，包含813名患者、三项锁定任务和六个基线模型。研究发现当前单时间点组织NGS数据无法完成有效预测，明确指向需要连续ctDNA数据作为输入模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥希替尼耐药是靶向治疗下克隆演化的典型范例，但缺乏公开的纵向患者轨迹基准数据集来训练或评估计算模型。

Method: 从MSK-CHORD、AACR Project GENIE BPC NSCLC和FLAURA三个真实世界临床基因组数据源中整合813名患者，定义三项任务（12个月进展二分类、进展时间回归、耐药机制六分类），提供六种基线模型（多数类预测器、逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LSTM、多任务Transformer）。

Result: 所有模型在单时间点特征下均无法超越随机水平，表明限制源于输入模态（单次组织NGS而非连续ctDNA）。TP53共突变使12个月进展率从29%升至59%，与文献一致。

Conclusion: OncoTraj建立了可复现、防泄漏的基准，将模态限制转化为对连续ctDNA富集版v2的具体设计需求。

Abstract: Resistance to first-line osimertinib in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer (NSCLC) is the canonical example of predictable clonal evolution under therapeutic pressure, yet no public benchmark exists for training or evaluating computational models on the corresponding longitudinal patient trajectories. We introduce OncoTraj, a public benchmark of 813 EGFR-mutant NSCLC patients receiving first-line osimertinib, harmonized from three real-world clinical-genomic sources: MSK-CHORD (672 patients), AACR Project GENIE BPC NSCLC (34 patients), and the FLAURA molecular-resistance supplement (107 patients). OncoTraj defines three locked tasks: (A) binary classification of progression by a fixed 12-month landmark, (B) regression of time-to-first-progression in days, and (C) six-class classification of the dominant resistance mechanism. We release the harmonized dataset, patient-level train/validation/test splits with an audited no-leakage guarantee, an open-source evaluation harness, and six reference baselines spanning a majority-class predictor, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, an LSTM, and a multi-task transformer. With v1's single-timepoint snapshot features, no task clears chance on clean within-source evaluation: the uniformity of this ceiling across every model class localizes the limit to the input modality (single-snapshot tissue NGS rather than serial ctDNA), not the algorithm. The benchmark does recover a reproducible literature-consistent association: TP53 co-mutation raises the 12-month progression rate from 29% to 59% cohort-wide. OncoTraj establishes a reproducible, leakage-audited baseline and converts the modality limit into concrete design requirements for a serial-ctDNA-enriched v2.

</details>


### [185] [Efficiently Learning Drifting Halfspaces with Massart Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11149)
*Mingchen Ma,Guyang Cao,Jelena Diakonikolas,Ilias Diakonikolas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在马萨特噪声下学习漂移概念的问题，针对间隔可分离线性分类器（半空间）提出了计算高效的在线学习算法，实现了η + Õ(Δ^{1/3}/γ)的错误率，并证明了Δ^{1/3}尺度在低次多项式检验下是不可避免的，揭示了信息-计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在概念漂移和标签噪声同时存在的复杂环境下，如何高效学习半空间分类器，并理解该问题的计算复杂度边界。

Method: 提出计算高效的在线学习算法，通过适应性的技术改进实可设情形下的错误率；同时利用低次多项式检验理论证明下界，展示信息-计算权衡。

Result: 上界：错误率η + Õ(Δ^{1/3}/γ)；下界：Δ^{1/3}尺度对于低次多项式检验是不可回避的，而信息论最优误差为Δ^{1/2}尺度。

Conclusion: 所提出算法的性能在计算意义上是最优的，揭示了漂移概念学习中的信息-计算权衡，即信息论上可实现更优误差（Δ^{1/2}），但计算上必须付出Δ^{1/3}的代价。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a drifting concept in the presence of Massart noise. In this framework, an online learner has access to a history of independent samples whose labels are noisy versions of a target concept that may change from round to round. The goal is to output, in each round, a hypothesis with small prediction error. We study the complexity of this learning problem for the fundamental class of margin-separable linear classifiers (halfspaces). On the positive side, we give a computationally efficient learner achieving error $η+ \tilde O(Δ^{1/3}/γ)$, where $η$ upper bounds the Massart noise rate, $Δ$ is the drift rate, and $γ$ is the margin. Interestingly, in the realizable setting, an adaptation of our techniques yields an efficient learner with an improved error rate over prior work. On the lower-bound side, we provide formal evidence of an information-computation tradeoff, strongly suggesting that our algorithm's performance is essentially optimal. Specifically, while the information-theoretically optimal error scales with $Δ^{1/2}$, we prove that $Δ^{1/3}$-scaling is unavoidable for low-degree polynomial tests, even in the special case of random classification noise.

</details>


### [186] [COGENT: Continuous Graph Emulators with Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for Long-Term Physical Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11162)
*Zesheng Liu,Maryam Rahnemoonfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COGENT，一种基于神经常微分方程的连续图仿真器，用于不规则地理网格上的长期物理预测。通过图历史编码器、连续潜变量神经ODE和残差解码器，实现对任意未来时刻的预测，并在瞬态冰盖模拟任务上展现出优于自回归图基线方法的长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于网格的物理仿真方法通常受限于固定时间离散化，缺乏在任意未来时刻查询系统状态的能力；同时自回归图基线方法在长期预测中存在稳定性问题。

Method: 1) 图历史编码器：对有限历史系统状态、关联力场和外部强迫进行编码，生成节点级上下文向量；2) 连续潜变量神经常微分方程(Neural ODE)：由上下文向量初始化并条件化，由插值的未来强迫和显式相对展开时间驱动；3) 残差解码器：将潜轨迹映射回未来物理状态，实现直接多步预测；4) 渐进展开时域采样与调度策略以稳定长期训练。

Result: 在冰盖与海平面系统模型(ISSM)生成的瞬态冰盖仿真上，COGENT相较于自回归图基线方法展现出更优的长期预测稳定性。

Conclusion: 连续图神经常微分方程为不规则地理网格上的可扩展物理预测提供了有前景的方法，特别适用于需要稳定长期预测和任意时间状态查询的应用场景。

Abstract: In this work, we present COGENT, a continuous graph emulator with Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for long-term physical forecasting on irregular geospatial meshes. COGENT encodes a finite history of system states and associated forcing fields and external forcings with a graph-based history encoder, producing node-wise context vectors that capture both local spatial interactions and temporal evolution. These context vectors initialize and condition a latent Neural Ordinary Differential Equation whose dynamics are driven by interpolated future forcings and explicit relative rollout time. By modeling the forecast trajectory as a continuous latent dynamical system, COGENT can generate predictions at arbitrary future times rather than being restricted to a fixed temporal discretization. A residual decoder maps the resulting latent trajectories back to future physical states, enabling direct multi-step forecasting without repeatedly feeding predicted states back into the model. This formulation combines graph-based spatial representation, history-conditioned latent dynamics, and continuous-time rollout in a unified framework for mesh-based physical simulation emulation. In order to stabilize training with long-horizon supervision, we also propose effective rollout-horizon sampling and a progressive rollout-horizon scheduling strategy. We evaluate COGENT on transient ice-sheet simulations generated by the Ice-sheet and Sea-level System Model, demonstrating improved long-range stability over autoregressive graph baselines. These results suggest that continuous graph Neural ODEs provide a promising methodology for scalable physical forecasting on irregular geospatial meshes, particularly in applications that require stable long-horizon predictions and the ability to query system states at arbitrary times.

</details>


### [187] [Predicting Future Behaviors in Reasoning Models Enables Better Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11172)
*Evgenii Kortukov,Piotr Komorowski,Florian Klein,Paula Engl,Gabriele Sarti,Seong Joon Oh,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过训练激活探针预测大推理模型（LRM）未来行为倾向，并基于此设计FPCG文本级引导方法，在几乎不降低输出质量的前提下实现了比传统激活引导更有效的模型行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时引导方法（如激活引导）依赖于检测模型已生成文本中行为的内部特征，但这些检测特征对未来行为预测能力差，且容易降低输出质量。因此需要寻找更好的干预目标。

Method: 训练激活探针从中间推理步骤预测未来行为倾向（准确率64%-91%），然后基于这些预测特征提出FPCG方法：采样多个候选句子，根据探针预测的未来行为可能性选择最佳输出。

Result: FPCG在几乎不损失输出质量的情况下实现有效引导，并在多个激活引导失败的评估场景中成功引导；探针预测未来行为的准确率达64%-91%。

Conclusion: 区分检测特征和预测特征能实现更精细的LRM行为控制方法。

Abstract: Deployed large reasoning models (LRMs) often behave unexpectedly. Test-time steering controls LRM outputs by intervening on their hidden representations, but it can degrade output quality. We argue that prior steering work implicitly relies on internal features that detect behavior in already generated text. We show that these detection features are poor predictors of future behavioral outcomes, and thus not the natural intervention target. Instead, we train activation probes to predict future behavior likelihoods from intermediate reasoning steps. These probes predict the most likely behavior with 64%-91% accuracy, revealing a separate type of internal prediction features. Building on these prediction features, we introduce a text-level steering method, Future Probe Controlled Generation. FPCG samples multiple candidate sentences and chooses the best one according to a probe predicting the future behavior likelihood. This enables steering with almost no output quality degradation. FPCG also enables steering in several evaluations where activation steering fails. These results show that distinguishing detection and prediction features enables a more nuanced approach to controlling LRM behaviors.

</details>


### [188] [When to Align, When to Predict: A Phase Diagram for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11190)
*Ilay Kamai,Hugues Van Assel,Aviv Regev,Hagai B. Perets,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的线性框架来系统理解多模态学习中跨模态对齐（CA）和跨模态预测（CP）何时有效、何时失败。通过带结构化跨模态扰扰相关性的尖峰信号加噪声模型，推导出两种目标的分离比，揭示互补失效模式，并构建了包含四个模态学习阶段的相图（Both、CA only、CP only、Neither）。实验验证了该框架在非线性情境下的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对跨模态对齐和跨模态预测在多模态表示学习中何时成功、何时失败的系统性理解，导致在科学领域（如生物医学、天体物理学）中，从业者无法诊断为何标准方法表现不如最优单模态。

Method: 1. 构建统一的线性框架，基于尖峰信号加噪声模型和结构化跨模态扰扰相关性。2. 推导对齐和预测的分离比，揭示两者互补的失效模式：对齐会白化每个模态并在扰扰跨视图强相关时失效；预测通过单侧白化编码可跨模态预测的信息，恢复质量由源模态质量决定。3. 构建四阶段相图。4. 提出数据驱动流程，利用少量标注子样本将真实数据集定位到相图中。

Result: 在合成数据、立体视觉基准、图像-字幕对和真实天体物理数据上的实验验证了非线性情境下的预测结果，包括跨模态训练可能有害的"Neither"阶段。

Conclusion: 该框架允许从业者在训练前诊断多模态问题并选择正确目标，在跨模态训练前即可确定最优目标及预测方向。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment (CA) and cross-modal prediction (CP) are the dominant paradigms for multimodal representation learning, yet there is no systematic understanding of when each succeeds, when each fails, and when cross-modal training helps at all -- a gap that leaves practitioners, especially in scientific domains like biomedicine or astrophysics, with heterogeneous instruments and multiple levels of organization and measurement, unable to diagnose why standard methods underperform the best single modality. We develop a unified linear framework that addresses both questions. Under a spiked signal-plus-noise model with structured cross-modal nuisance correlation, we derive separation ratios for both objectives that expose complementary failure modes: alignment whitens each modality and fails when nuisance is strongly correlated across views; prediction encodes whatever is cross-predictable through a one-sided whitening, with recovery governed by source-modality quality. The resulting phase diagram partitions multimodal problems into four regimes: Both, CA only, CP only, and Neither. We present a data-driven procedure to locate real-world datasets in this diagram using a small labeled subsample, identifying the preferred objective and prediction direction before any cross-modal training. Experiments on synthetic data, stereo-vision benchmarks, image-caption pairs, and real astrophysical data validate the predictions in the nonlinear regime, including the Neither regime where cross-modal training is actively harmful. Our framework lets practitioners diagnose their multimodal problem and choose the right objective before committing to training. Code to reproduce the results is available at https://github.com/IlayMalinyak/mm_align_vs_pred.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [An algorithm for dynamical quantum optimal transport with applications to quantum chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10075)
*Genevieve Dusson,Virginie Ehrlacher,Etienne Obermeyer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文数值研究基于Benamou-Brenier动力学的量子最优输运距离族，提出内点正则化方法计算正半定矩阵间的测地线，并展示其在量子化学近似中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子最优输运是快速发展的领域，需要数值方法研究密度矩阵空间上的距离及其在量子化学等应用中的表现。

Method: 采用基于Benamou-Brenier动力学的动态QOT公式，引入内点正则化方法计算正半定矩阵间的测地线，通过积分核和密度进行可视化。

Result: 动态QOT通过适当参数调优可良好近似某些量子化学问题；研究了距离的数值性质以及矩阵规模增大时对象的收敛性。

Conclusion: 动态量子最优输运在量子化学中具有应用潜力，且数值方法具有良好的收敛性质。

Abstract: Quantum optimal transport (QOT) is a rapidly developing field. Among the many formulations of this adaptation of classical optimal transport (OT) to spaces of density matrices, we numerically study a family of distances based on a dynamical formulation inspired by the Benamou-Brenier OT formulation. We introduce an interior-point regularized method to compute geodesics between positive semidefinite matrices and visualize the results in terms of integral kernels and densities, inspired by quantum chemistry applications. We show that dynamical QOT may provide a good approximation to certain problems in quantum chemistry with appropriate parameter tuning. We also study the numerical properties of the distances at hand, and the convergence of the objects when the size of the matrices increases.

</details>


### [190] [Branch and Price for Railway Crew Scheduling: Benchmark Instances and Computational Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10081)
*Bart van Rossum*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种先进的列生成分支定价算法，并创建了包含75个基准实例的铁路乘务调度问题库。实验表明，原始启发式算法能有效将最优性差距控制在1%以下，而分支规则和减价固定技术贡献有限，改进对偶界仍是精确方法的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路乘务调度是运营商面临的关键规划问题，但精确的分支定价方法研究不足，且缺乏公开可用的基准测试实例。

Method: 提出了先进的分支定价算法，系统研究了分支规则、原始启发式和减价固定技术；通过模拟完整铁路规划流程生成了75个基准实例（450-3016个任务），并基于荷兰铁路数据进行了验证。

Result: 所有规模实例的平均最优性差距远低于1%，原始启发式是缩小差距的主要驱动力，而分支规则和减价固定贡献甚微。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持了基于列生成的原始启发式在铁路乘务调度中的有效性，同时指出改进对偶界仍是精确方法面临的主要挑战。

Abstract: Railway crew scheduling consists of assigning a set of tasks to crew members in the form of feasible duties so as to minimise costs, and is one of the key planning problems faced by railway operators. While column generation is the dominant solution method, literature on exact branch-and-price approaches is scarce. Moreover, few publicly available benchmark instances exist. We present a state-of-the-art branch-and-price algorithm and conduct a systematic computational study of its components, including branching rules, primal heuristics, and reduced cost fixing techniques. To this end, we introduce a novel library of 75 benchmark instances, ranging in size from 450 to 3,016 tasks. The instances are generated by mimicking the full railway planning process, validated against data from Netherlands Railways, and publicly available with best known solutions and a solution checker. We report average optimality gaps well below 1% across all instance sizes, driven primarily by the strong performance of primal heuristics, whereas branching and reduced cost fixing contribute little to closing the gap. Our findings provide support for the popularity of column generation-based primal heuristics, and show that improving the dual bound remains the main challenge for exact methods in railway crew scheduling.

</details>


### [191] [The Dual Averaging Power-Prox Method with Application to Heavy-Tail Incremental Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10110)
*Yuan Gao,Jeremy Rack,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限和复合优化问题，在增量梯度访问和重尾梯度噪声两个偏离经典SGD理论的条件下，提出了增量梯度的 Dual Averaging Power-Prox 方法，并建立了该设定下首个收敛性分析，证明其渐近收敛率优于带替换采样的SGD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SGD理论的假设（如独立同分布采样、轻尾噪声）与当下ML训练实践存在显著差距，实际中常用固定循环遍历组件梯度（增量梯度）且梯度噪声常呈现重尾分布。本文旨在弥合这一理论与实践的鸿沟，为更贴近实际训练的设置提供理论保证。

Method: 提出了针对增量梯度的 Dual Averaging Power-Prox 方法。该方法结合了对偶平均（Dual Averaging）和 Power-Prox 技术，用于处理固定循环遍历组件梯度（非随机采样）以及重尾梯度噪声（分量梯度在最优点的q阶中心矩有界，q∈(1,2]）。

Result: 在该设定下建立了首个收敛性分析，并证明了所提方法的渐近收敛率优于带替换采样的对应SGD方法。

Conclusion: Dual Averaging Power-Prox方法在增量梯度访问和重尾噪声设定下不仅可行，而且能获得比经典SGD更好的渐近收敛性能，为实践中广泛使用的固定循环梯度更新提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study finite-sum composite optimization under two departures from classical stochastic gradient descent theory that are central in practice: incremental gradient access and heavy-tailed gradient noise. Specifically, we consider fixed cyclic passes over component gradients and assume that, at the optimum, component gradients have a bounded $q$-th centralized moment for some $q\in(1,2]$. This setting is much closer to modern ML training practice than the assumptions used in classical SGD theory, yet its theoretical understanding remains limited. We propose a Dual Averaging Power-Prox method for incremental gradients and establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first convergence analysis in this regime. We further show that our method achieves a better asymptotic convergence rate than the corresponding SGD method with i.i.d. (with-replacement) sampling.

</details>


### [192] [Bidirectional SDDP with dimension-free complexity for solving strongly convex stochastic dynamic programming equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10161)
*Vincent Guigues,Pablo Barros*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了双向随机对偶动态规划（BSDDP）算法在强凸多阶段随机优化问题中的复杂度，建立了达到ε-最优策略所需迭代次数的期望上界，该界依赖于强凸常数和Lipschitz常数，但与状态空间维度无关，优于SDDP算法在凸问题上的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDDP算法的复杂度分析主要针对凸问题，其复杂度受状态空间维度影响。本文旨在分析BSDDP算法在强凸成本函数下的复杂度，并证明其维度无关性，从而在状态维度大、阶段数适中的场景下提供更优的理论保证。

Method: 在标准正则性假设（无折扣因子）下，基于强凸成本函数和Lipschitz连续性条件，推导BSDDP算法期望迭代次数的显式复杂度上界，并与SDDP算法（Lan, 2020）进行理论对比。

Result: 复杂度上界与状态空间维度无关，仅依赖于各阶段成本函数的强凸常数和Lipschitz常数；当成本函数强凸性增强时，复杂度降低；在阶段数适中、状态维度大的场景下，BSDDP的复杂度优于SDDP。

Conclusion: BSDDP算法在处理强凸多阶段随机优化问题时具备维度无关的复杂度保证，在结构化问题实例中效率更高，为大规模高维随机优化问题提供了理论更优的算法选择。

Abstract: We analyze the complexity of Bidirectional Stochastic Dual Dynamic Programming (BSDDP) algorithm applied to multistage stochastic optimization problems with strongly convex cost functions. Under standard regularity assumptions and without discount factor, we establish an explicit complexity bound on the expected number of iterations needed to obtain an epsilon-optimal policy. Our bound depends on the strong convexity constants and Lipschitz constants of the stagewise cost functions but is independent of the dimensionality of the state space. This improves upon the complexity of SDDP for convex problems from (Lan, 2020), particularly when the number of stages is moderate and the state dimension is large. The analysis further reveals that the complexity decreases as the strong convexity of the cost functions increases, highlighting the algorithm efficiency in structured problem instances.

</details>


### [193] [Submodular Optimization with Applications to Decision and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10192)
*Shamak Dutta,Bahman Gharesifard,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了次模集函数优化在系统与控制领域的理论、算法与应用，涵盖次模函数的曲率与次模率等结构性质、三类常见约束族（拟阵、背包、p-系统）、单调与非单调次模最大化的近似算法及最新近似比与困难性结果，并详细综述了传感器调度、多智能体协同、鲁棒优化、主从系统、分布式优化、博弈论、系统理论、资源分配、社交网络与信息路径规划等应用方向。特别指出某些控制论经典目标函数（如可控性格拉姆矩阵的对数行列式与秩、卡尔曼滤波信息矩阵的对数行列式）具有次模性，而稳态卡尔曼滤波误差协方差与逆格拉姆矩阵的平均控制能量则不满足次模或超模性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次模集函数具有收益递减特性，为决策与控制中许多子集选择问题提供了统一的组合优化框架。尽管精确最大化通常是NP难的，次模函数的结构性质使得简单的贪心算法能够实现常数因子近似保证。鉴于系统与控制领域大量问题可建模为次模优化，有必要系统梳理相关理论、算法及应用。

Method: 采用综述方法，系统梳理次模函数的结构性质（曲率、次模率等）、约束族（拟阵、背包、p-系统）、单调与非单调次模最大化的贪心及其变体算法与近似比，并跨领域归纳传感器调度、多智能体协同、鲁棒优化、主从系统、分布式优化、博弈论、控制理论、资源分配、社交网络与信息路径规划中的应用。

Result: 阐明了次模函数在系统与控制中的核心地位：可控性格拉姆矩阵的对数行列式与秩、卡尔曼滤波信息矩阵的对数行列式具有次模性；而稳态卡尔曼滤波误差协方差和逆格拉姆矩阵的平均控制能量不满足次模或超模性质。提供了最新的近似比与困难性结果，并强调了基于曲率和次模率的实例依赖性改进。

Conclusion: 次模优化为系统与控制中的子集选择问题提供了高效且具有理论保证的贪心算法框架。某些经典控制目标函数具有次模性，而另一些则不具备，这为后续研究指明了方向。综述指出若干跨领域的开放性问题有待进一步探索。

Abstract: Submodular set functions, characterized by the diminishing-returns property, provide a unifying combinatorial framework for many subset-selection problems in decision and control. Although exact maximization is NP-hard in general, the structural properties of submodular functions enable simple greedy algorithms that achieve constant-factor approximation guarantees for monotone objectives, with randomized greedy-based variants extending such guarantees to the non-monotone case. This survey reviews the theory, algorithms, and applications of submodular optimization with a focus on systems and control. We cover the structural properties of submodular functions, including curvature and the submodularity ratio, the constraint families that arise in practice (matroids, knapsack, and $p$-systems), and the main approximation algorithms for monotone and non-monotone submodular maximization, with up-to-date approximation ratios and hardness results. We then survey applications across sensor scheduling, multi-agent coordination, robust submodular optimization, leader-follower systems, distributed submodular optimization, game theory, system theory, resource allocation, social networks, and informative path planning. The survey emphasizes practically implementable greedy-based algorithms and instance-dependent refinements via curvature and the submodularity ratio. We close with observations on canonical control-theoretic objectives: certain functionals are submodular (the log-determinant and rank of the controllability Gramian, and the log-determinant of the Kalman filter information matrix), whereas closely related objectives fail to be sub- or supermodular (the steady-state Kalman filter error covariance, and the average control energy obtained from the inverse Gramian). We also highlight the cross-cutting open directions that follow.

</details>


### [194] [A dual dynamic programming approach with dimension-free complexity for a class of convex optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10203)
*Pablo Barros,Vincent Guigues,Jiaming Liang,Renato Monteiro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究对偶动态规划（DDP）方法在求解一类具有线性耦合约束的凸优化问题时的复杂度，提出了一种维度无关的复杂度界，并设计了两种变体（多割和两割DDP），其中两割变体在保持理论保证的同时更节省内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DDP复杂度结果依赖于状态向量的维度，且随维度指数增长，本文旨在建立与维度无关的复杂度界。

Method: 首先研究无约束强凸问题，建立灵活框架FDDP，获得维度无关的迭代复杂度界；随后通过光滑化和罚函数将原始线性约束问题转化为无约束强凸问题，应用FDDP得到维度无关的复杂度界；借鉴束方法思想，FDDP通过通用过程更新成本函数的低阶近似，涵盖多割DDP和新提出的两割DDP变体。

Result: 建立了与维度无关的迭代复杂度界；两割变体每次迭代仅维护每阶段两个仿射割，内存效率更高且理论保证相同；数值实验展示了两种变体的实际表现及其对问题参数的依赖性。

Conclusion: 本文通过FDDP框架成功实现了DDP方法的维度无关复杂度分析，两割变体在保持理论优势的同时显著提升内存效率，为大规模线性约束凸优化问题提供了更实用的求解方案。

Abstract: This paper studies the complexity of a dual dynamic programming (DDP) method for solving a class of convex optimization problems with linear coupling constraints. Existing complexity results based on DDP depend on the dimensions of the state vectors and, in particular, grow exponentially with dimension. The goal of this paper is to establish a complexity bound that is independent of the dimension. Our approach first studies an unconstrained strongly convex problem and develops a flexible framework for DDP, called FDDP, for solving the associated dynamic programming equations, and establishes an iteration-complexity bound for it that is independent of the dimension. A dimension-independent complexity bound for the original linearly constrained problem is then obtained by applying FDDP to a corresponding unconstrained strongly convex problem obtained via smoothing and penalization. Inspired by the literature on bundle methods, FDDP updates lower approximations of cost-to-go functions through a generic procedure that includes both the classical multi-cut DDP and a new two-cut DDP variant as special cases. The two-cut variant maintains only two affine cuts per stage at each iteration, making it more memory-efficient while retaining the same theoretical guarantees. Finally, numerical experiments illustrate the practical behavior of multi-cut and two-cut DDP, including their dependence on problem parameters and their performance relative to a direct quadratic constraint reformulation.

</details>


### [195] [Constructive Versions of Ekeland's Variational Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10339)
*Douglas S. Bridges*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在度量空间上实值函数下截集的初步结果基础上，提供了埃克兰德近似优化定理的构造性对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Ekeland变分原理提供构造性（而非存在性）的证明框架，填补经典结果在可计算/构造性数学中的空白。

Method: 利用度量空间上实值函数的下截集（lower sections）的构造性性质，通过分析这些下截集的结构来构建近似优化点。

Result: 成功给出了Ekeland定理的构造性版本，证明了在适当的构造性假设下，存在满足Ekeland近似优化条件的点。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了构造性数学在变分分析中的应用，表明Ekeland变分原理可以在不依赖经典选择公理的情况下得到有效实现。

Abstract: Building on preliminary results about lower sections of real-valued functions on a metric space, we provide a constructive counterpart of Ekeland's theorem on the approximate optimisation of real-valued functions.

</details>


### [196] [Koopman Modeling and Stabilization of Discrete-Time Nonlinear Control Systems: Bilinearity on a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10344)
*Jarod Morris,Xiuzhen Ye,Wentao Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了非线性离散时间系统在满足光滑性条件下，可通过将状态和输入分别提升到再生核希尔伯特空间精确表示为双线性动力学，并利用核平方和形式的Lyapunov函数通过无限维优化解决镇定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman建模在存在输入变量的非线性系统中，由于即使在无限维也不存在完全线性时不变模型，导致Koopman控制方法至今基本处于开放问题状态。

Method: 1) 将状态变量和输入变量分别提升到再生核希尔伯特空间(RKHS)，采用线性-径向乘积核定义RKHS；2) 证明非线性系统可精确表示为双线性动力学；3) 镇定问题表述为核平方和形式的Lyapunov函数递减的无限维优化问题；4) 通过离散化方案求解状态依赖的条件概率测度。

Result: 满足适当光滑性和正则性条件的非线性离散时间系统可精确表示为双线性动力学形式，而非传统Koopman方法追求的完全线性形式。

Conclusion: 本文为Koopman控制提供了新的理论框架，通过双线性化而非线性化来处理非线性系统，将控制问题转化为可解的无限维优化问题。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of Koopman modeling for nonlinear systems, in the presence of input variables, the evident nonexistence of a fully linear time-invariant model even in infinite dimensions makes Koopman-based control largely an open problem to date. Focusing on discrete-time systems in this paper, which eschews from using operator semigroup and infinitesimal generator notions, it is proven that nonlinear systems, if satisfying appropriate smoothness and regularity conditions, can be expressed exactly as bilinear dynamics, when the state variables and input variables are separately lifted into their reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs). To account for the knowledge of an equilibrium point at the origin, the RKHS is defined by a linear--radial product kernel, and hence the functions belonging to this RKHS are spanned by the multiplications of component functions and Sobolev functions. The stabilization problem, namely the determination of a feedback law that causes a Lyapunov function (expressed as a kernel sum-of-squares form) to decrease, is then posed as an infinite-dimensional optimization problem over state-dependent conditional probability measures over the input space, solved via a discretization scheme.

</details>


### [197] [Supermodularity and Submodularity in Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10345)
*Bo Zhou,Ruiwei Jiang,Siqian Shen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究双层网络阻断问题，分析了防御者在攻击者决策下的目标函数何时满足子模性或超模性，并利用这些性质实现数量级计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络阻断问题中，防御者的最优目标函数在攻击者阻断决策下的（超）子模性有助于更高效地求解双层整数规划，但现有研究缺乏对此性质成立条件的系统刻画。

Method: 针对最小成本流阻断问题推导了三种攻击类型（供需、流量容量、成本系数）下子模性与超模性的充要条件；推广到设施选址、最大流和最短路阻断问题；引入网络参数和特殊拓扑以建立更宽松的条件；进一步考虑防御者含二值决策（修复/加固）时保持或恢复（超）子模性的条件。

Result: 所推导的条件仅依赖于攻击位置，适用于一般网络拓扑与参数设置；在系列-并联网络中给出了最短路和最大流阻断问题的充要条件；数值实验表明利用这些性质生成有效不等式可实现数量级计算加速。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了网络阻断问题中目标函数（超）子模性的成立条件，为大规模网络阻断问题的高效求解提供了理论依据和实用方法。

Abstract: We study a bilevel network interdiction problem, with an attacker interdicting (attacking) certain components of a network and a defender optimizing operations over the ensuing network. We study when the defender's optimal objective is submodular or supermodular with respect to the attacker's interdiction decisions, for optimizing the bilevel integer program more efficiently. We first consider the min-cost flow (MinCF) interdiction problem and derive necessary and sufficient conditions for the supermodularity or submodularity to hold under three types of attacks, respectively on supplies/demands, flow capacities, and cost coefficients. We extend to other variants, including capacitated facility location, maximum flow (MaxF), and shortest path (SP) interdiction. The conditions hold under general network topologies and parameter settings, and depend solely on the locations of the attacks. We further incorporate additional network information (e.g., detailed parameters and special topologies) to establish less restrictive conditions. We also derive necessary and sufficient conditions for supermodularity or submodularity in SP and MaxF interdiction in series-parallel networks. Furthermore, we explore more challenging interdiction problems where the defender may make additional binary decisions (e.g., repairing or reinforcing the network) and identify conditions that preserve submodularity or recover supermodularity. Via extensive numerical studies with diverse types of attacks, we demonstrate an order-of-magnitude computational speedup achieved by exploiting these properties and generating valid inequalities, for solving network interdiction at scale.

</details>


### [198] [A stochastic gradient algorithm for non-separable optimization with convergence guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10383)
*Yingzhou Li,Ruofan Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种处理非可分离目标的SGD框架，使用两种批量梯度（理想梯度G和缓存替代梯度H），在温和假设下证明了局部线性收敛和次线性收敛，并给出了关键超参数对收敛影响的显式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGD理论主要针对样本可分离目标，而现实中的损失函数常依赖于数据集级非可分离项（如对比学习、排序学习等），缺乏统一的收敛性分析框架。

Method: 构建SGD风格框架，引入两个批量梯度构造：理想逐批次梯度G和缓存替代梯度H（用于全数据项计算昂贵场景）。在平滑性和Jacobian有界性假设下，分别对G驱动和H驱动更新进行局部收敛分析。

Result: 在局部强凸条件下证明线性收敛，在局部凸条件下证明O(1/k)次线性收敛；这些保证对固定步长成立，且显式刻画了缓存陈旧度、替代梯度近似误差、批量大小和步长对收敛常数及步长可行域的影响。

Conclusion: 提出的框架统一了可分离与非可分离目标的SGD分析，标准小批量SGD是其特例，为实际中处理非可分离目标提供了理论保障和超参数选择指导。

Abstract: We study non-separable objectives in which the loss depend on dataset-level quantities. We introduce an SGD-style framework that employs two batch-gradient constructs: the ideal per-batch gradient `$G$' and a cached surrogate `$H$' for cases where full-data terms are expensive.
  Notably, in the sample-wise separable case, our method reduces to standard mini-batch SGD. Our main contribution is a unified local convergence theory: under mild smoothness and Jacobian-boundedness assumptions,
  we prove local linear convergence under local strong convexity and local $O(1/k)$ sublinear convergence under local convexity for both `$G$'-driven and `$H$'-driven updates.
  Crucially, these guarantees hold for fixed step sizes within explicitly characterized ranges; we provide explicit bounds showing how cache staleness, surrogate approximation error, batch size, and step size influence the convergence constants and allowable step-size ranges.

</details>


### [199] [A Mean-Field Analysis of Multi-Head Self-Attention under Cross-Entropy Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10469)
*Cheng Huan,Hongfwei Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为单层因果多头自注意力模型建立了平均场理论框架，将每个注意力头视为相空间粒子，在无限头极限下推导出Wasserstein梯度流方程，证明了有限头近似界、全局极小化特征、传播混沌估计，并研究了PDE的长时间行为（能量耗散、收敛性、指数稳定性等），为注意力头训练提供了严格的平均场基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将平均场理论从传统浅层网络（平方损失回归）扩展到包含softmax交叉熵损失和查询-键-值结构的因果多头自注意力模型，填补该方向的理论空白。

Method: 将每个注意力头视为相空间粒子，以头的经验分布作为大头状态变量；在无限头极限下定义关于概率测度的风险泛函，其一阶变分生成非线性Wasserstein梯度流方程；结合变分分析、梯度流理论和传播混沌技术。

Result: 证明了静态有限头近似界、全局极小化的变分支撑条件、有限头SGD与极限PDE之间的定量有限时间传播混沌估计；建立了PDE长时间行为（能量耗散、收敛到平稳集、收敛率）；给出了Dirac平稳测度的局部指数稳定性与不稳定性判据。

Conclusion: 为注意力头训练提供了严格的平均场基准框架，阐明了从平稳性到收敛性和稳定性所需的额外紧致性、景观和曲率假设。

Abstract: This paper develops a mean-field theory for a simplified single-layer causal multi-head self-attention model trained by cross-entropy minimization. Each attention head is treated as a particle in parameter space, and the empirical law of the heads is used as the large-head state variable. In the infinite-head limit, the averaged attention logits define a risk functional on probability measures, whose first variation generates a nonlinear Wasserstein gradient-flow equation. Unlike classical mean-field analyses of shallow networks that often focus on square-loss regression, the present model contains the softmax residual from the cross-entropy objective and the query-key-value structure of masked self-attention. We prove a static finite-head approximation bound for the optimal risk, characterize global minimizers through a variational support condition, and establish a quantitative finite-time propagation-of-chaos estimate comparing finite-head stochastic gradient descent with the limiting PDE. We then study the long-time behavior of the PDE: energy dissipation, convergence to the stationary set under compactness, convergence to a single stationary measure under topological or Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz assumptions, and explicit convergence rates under gradient-domination conditions. Finally, we prove local exponential stability under a Wasserstein strong-monotonicity condition and give verifiable stability and instability criteria for Dirac stationary measures. The results provide a rigorous baseline mean-field framework for attention-head training and clarify the additional compactness, landscape, and curvature assumptions needed to pass from stationarity to convergence and stability.

</details>


### [200] [A Nonlinear Snell Envelope Representation for Path-Dependent Controller-Stopper Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10494)
*Magnus Perninge*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域零和随机微分博弈，其中一方控制路径依赖随机系统，另一方可以提前终止博弈。通过控制随机化方法，证明上下价值函数相等，并将共同博弈价值表示为非线性Snell包络，其基础停过程由带约束跳跃的BSDE的唯一最大解给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一方可提前终止博弈的零和随机微分博弈中，上下价值函数不一致的问题，建立博弈价值的存在性与表示。

Method: 引入控制随机化（control randomization）方法，将博弈转化为更易处理的形式；利用带约束跳跃的BSDE的最大解刻画停过程。

Result: 证明了上下价值函数相等，建立了共同博弈价值的存在性；获得了博弈价值的非线性Snell包络表示。

Conclusion: 控制随机化方法可有效处理一方拥有提前终止权的随机微分博弈，为路径依赖系统的博弈问题提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We consider a finite-horizon, zero-sum stochastic differential game in which one player controls a path-dependent stochastic system, while the opponent is given the opportunity to terminate the game prematurely. We introduce a control randomization formulation, which allows us to establish that the upper and lower value functions coincide.
  Our approach also yields a representation of the common game value in terms of a nonlinear Snell envelope, where the underlying stopped process is given by the unique maximal solution of a backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with constrained jumps.

</details>


### [201] [Short-Range IAM Operations to Overcome Access Gaps Around Bologna Airport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10552)
*Jean-Claude Lebègue,Xuhao Gui,Daniel Delahaye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究创新航空运输（IAM）作为博洛尼亚机场的枢纽 feeder 和疏散服务，通过时间扩展网络模型和两阶段优化方法（A* 路径规划 + 动态规划车队调度），模拟了 IAM 支线网络对机场可达性的影响，发现 IAM 在多数线路上相比铁路具有显著时间优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统固定翼航班在短程机场间连接效率低下、地面交通耗时长的问题，探索 IAM 作为枢纽 feeder 和疏散服务的可行性，以增强机场区域可达性而不改变现有航空公司时刻表。

Method: 构建 IAM 专用时间扩展网络（机场作为 vertiports），采用两阶段顺序求解：第一步使用 A* 算法进行乘客路径规划以揭示边缘需求压力，第二步使用动态规划调度有限 IAM 车队。需求生成基于 BLQ 航班时刻表，使用交错滚动窗口模型，考虑 20 分钟缓冲期和 70% "停留" 份额，并通过重力模型分配到邻近机场。

Result: 使用 2024 年 5 月 5 日 OAG 时刻表模拟 09:00-17:00，50 架 10 座 IAM 飞行器（30 m/s），5 分钟时间窗。多数线路 IAM 比铁路具有大幅时间优势（如 BLQ-Il Ciocco: IAM 46 分钟 vs. 火车 237-294 分钟），少数线路铁路仍有竞争力（如 BLQ-佛罗伦萨）。敏感性分析表明飞行速度和停留份额显著影响覆盖范围、时序可行性和枢纽可达性。

Conclusion: IAM 有潜力在不改变航空公司时刻表的前提下加强博洛尼亚机场的区域覆盖能力，技术参数和乘客采用率是影响其可行性的关键因素。

Abstract: We study Innovative Air Mobility (IAM) as a dedicated hub feeder and dispersal service for Bologna Guglielmo Marconi Airport (BLQ), addressing short-range airport-to-airport connectivity where scheduled fixed-wing services are inefficient and surface access is time-consuming. Demand is generated directly from BLQ's flight timetable via a staggered rolling-window model: arriving passengers become IAM-eligible after a 20-minute buffer (a baseline 70% ''staying'' share is considered and distributed across nearby airports using a gravity model), while feeder demand for departures is timestamped backward by IAM travel time plus a 2-hour pre-flight buffer. We build an IAM-only time-expanded network (airports as vertiports) and solve two sequential subproblems: passenger routing with A* to reveal edge-level demand pressure, followed by fleet dispatch with dynamic programming to schedule a finite IAM fleet. Using OAG schedules for 5 May 2024, we simulate 09:00-17:00 with a 50-vehicle fleet (10 seats, 30 m/s) and 5-minute windows. Results show large travel-time advantages versus rail for many links (e.g., BLQ-Il Ciocco: 46 min IAM vs. 237-294 min by train), while a few pairs remain competitive for rail (e.g., BLQ-Florence). Sensitivity to IAM speed and staying share quantifies how technology and adoption shape feasible coverage, timing feasibility, and hub accessibility, demonstrating the potential of IAM to strengthen BLQ's regional reach without altering airline schedules.

</details>


### [202] [Accelerating SAV-based optimization via randomized low-rank Hessian approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10562)
*Ryo Sagawa,Daisuke Furihata,Yuto Miyatake*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Nyström增强的松弛标量辅助变量方法（N-RSAV），通过融入曲率信息加速收敛，同时保持无条件修正能量耗散律，在病态问题（如PINNs训练）上显著优于传统RSAV方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSAV方法仅依赖一阶信息，在病态问题（如PINNs）中收敛缓慢，亟需引入二阶曲率信息加速收敛。

Method: 利用随机低秩Nyström近似获取近似Hessian信息设计RSAV线性算子，通过特征值截断保证半正定性以维持耗散结构，并引入基于能量偏差的自适应策略复用近似Hessian以降低计算成本。

Result: 在凸二次问题和PINNs训练等具有有效低秩结构的病态问题上，N-RSAV比传统RSAV方法实现显著更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持无条件能量耗散的前提下有效加速了收敛，并为RSAV框架提供了在PL条件下的收敛性分析。

Abstract: We propose a new optimization method, the Nyström-enhanced relaxed scalar auxiliary variable method (N-RSAV), which incorporates curvature information into the RSAV framework to accelerate convergence while preserving an unconditional modified energy dissipation law. Existing RSAV-based methods rely solely on first-order information and often suffer from slow convergence, particularly for ill-conditioned problems such as those arising in physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). To address this limitation, we design the linear operator in the RSAV scheme using approximate Hessian information obtained from a randomized low-rank Nyström approximation. To preserve the dissipation structure, we enforce positive semidefiniteness through eigenvalue truncation. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive strategy that reuses the approximate Hessian based on the deviation between the original and modified energies, significantly reducing computational cost. We also provide a convergence analysis of the RSAV scheme with a general positive semidefinite operator under the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition and establish corresponding convergence guarantees for N-RSAV under the PL condition and an additional convexity assumption. Numerical experiments on ill-conditioned problems with effectively low-rank structure, including convex quadratic problems and training of PINNs, demonstrate that the proposed methods achieve substantially faster convergence than conventional RSAV-based approaches.

</details>


### [203] [Direct Data-Driven Approximate Optimal Control of Nonlinear Input-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10810)
*Benita Nortmann,Thulasi Mylvaganam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将数据驱动系统表示与非线性最优控制求解框架相结合的方法，通过将未知动态嵌入扩展状态空间，利用纯数据依赖的代数条件刻画解，从而直接基于数据设计具有局部稳定性和性能保证的动态状态反馈控制器，无需显式辨识系统动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性最优控制方法通常依赖显式系统模型，而对于未知非线性系统，模型辨识成本高且误差累积影响控制性能。本文旨在提出一种无需显式辨识动态，直接利用数据设计控制器的方法。

Method: 将未知动态嵌入扩展状态空间，利用数据驱动的系统表示，将最优控制问题的解转化为纯数据依赖的代数条件，进而设计动态状态反馈控制器。

Result: 所提出的方法能够直接基于数据设计控制器，无需显式辨识系统动态，并具备局部稳定性和性能保证。

Conclusion: 本文验证了数据驱动方法在未知非线性输入仿射系统上的有效性，为无模型非线性最优控制提供了一种系统化框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we combine a data-driven system representation with a framework to systematically construct (approximate) solutions to nonlinear optimal control problems. By immersing the unknown dynamics into an extended state space, solutions are characterised via purely data-dependent algebraic conditions. This allows us to design dynamic state-feedback controllers with local stability and performance guarantees for unknown nonlinear, input-affine systems directly using data, without explicitly identifying the dynamics.

</details>


### [204] [A Stochastic Maximum Principle for Partially Observed Jump-Diffusion Systems with Counting-Process Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10844)
*Jie Xiong,Ying Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推导了部分可观测跳扩散系统（观测为多元计数过程）的随机最大值原理，通过参考测度变换将问题转化为条件分布上的完全观测控制问题，得到条件哈密顿驻留条件作为最优性必要条件，并将其应用于线性二次型金融执行问题，得到闭式Riccati反馈律，数值实验表明该控制器优于高斯和跳盲近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机最优控制理论对部分可观测跳扩散系统的研究不足，尤其是当观测为具有状态依赖强度的多元计数过程时，缺乏必要的最优性条件。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 引入参考概率测度将观测过程转化为单位泊松过程，将部分观测控制问题重构为条件分布上的完全观测控制问题，推导条件哈密顿驻留条件作为必要最优性原理。针对线性二次型问题，通过匹配系数得到闭式Riccati反馈律。

Result: 得到了闭式Riccati反馈律，数值实验表明该控制器能够捕捉非高斯滤波结构，在滤波精度上优于高斯近似和跳盲近似，闭环性能接近全信息基准。

Conclusion: 所提框架为处理状态依赖强度计数过程观测的部分可观测跳扩散系统提供了理论最优性条件，并在金融执行问题中验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper derives a stochastic maximum principle (SMP) for partially observed jump-diffusion systems whose observations are multivariate counting processes with state-dependent intensities. By introducing a reference probability measure under which the observation processes become unit Poisson processes, the partially observed control problem is rewritten as a fully observed control one on the conditional distribution, leading to a conditional Hamiltonian stationarity condition as a necessary optimality principle. We apply the framework to a linear-quadratic financial execution problem. Matching coefficients yields a closed-form Riccati feedback law driven by the conditional mean of the latent state. Numerical experiments with a particle-filter implementation demonstrate that the resulting controller captures the non-Gaussian filtering structure arising from both asymmetric jumps and the nonlinear counting-process observation mechanism, improves filtering accuracy over Gaussian and jump-blind approximations, and achieves closed-loop performance close to the full-information oracle.

</details>


### [205] [Dynamic inverse problems: Single-loop online algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10951)
*Jyrki Jauhiainen,Yassine Nabou,Tuomo Valkonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无限时间跨度动态逆问题的单循环在线优化方法，该方法通过用单循环梯度估计替代精确梯度，使标准遗憾分析在PDE约束的动态逆问题中依然成立，并在动态EIT数值实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态逆问题中，当监测快速流动（如EIT）时，传统方法无法在数据到达前求解PDE，需要一种不完整求解PDE但仍保持收敛性的在线方法。

Method: 提出单循环（single loop）方法：每次迭代只对前向PDE和伴随PDE执行单步或少数几步标准迭代求解器，得到单循环梯度估计，该估计满足标准光滑性且误差可求和，从而保证标准在线优化遗憾证明仍有效。

Result: 动态EIT数值实验验证了理论预测，展示了该方法在实时求解PDE约束动态逆问题中的潜力。

Conclusion: 单循环梯度估计方法可以扩展标准在线优化方法到PDE约束的动态逆问题，实现实时求解。

Abstract: We study efficient online methods for dynamic inverse problems with infinite time horizon. We concentrate, in particular, on problems whose forward model arises from a PDE. Our motivating application is flow monitoring with Electrical Impedance Tomography (EIT). The idea of such online methods is to take single steps of of standard optimisation algorithms, on each time index; each data frame. A predictor, based on problem dynamics, is used to transfer iterates one from time index to the next one. If we monitor a fast flow with a correspondingly fast measurement modality, such as EIT, basic methods are unable to solve the PDE before new data arrives. Our idea, then, is to not solve it, and instead, on each iteration, each time index, take single or few steps of standard iterative solvers towards the solution of both the PDE and an adjoint PDE. This is what ``single loop'' refers to. To the overall problem, we apply standard online optimisation methods, at the outside developed for exact gradients $\nabla E_k(x^k)$ of the iteration-dependent data fidelity $E_k$ that incorporates the PDE. We replace the gradient by a single-loop estimate $\tilde\grad E_k(x^k)$ that satisfies standard smoothness properties with summable errors. This allows standard regret proofs to go through. Our numerical experiments on dynamic EIT validate the theoretical predictions and highlight the potential of the proposed approach for the real-time solution of PDE-constrained dynamic inverse problems.

</details>


### [206] [Continuous Time Identification of Linear Systems: Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10963)
*Fatima J. Ghadieh,Mireille E. Broucke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经形态计算原则的模型识别框架，不使用神经网络，而是在连续时间中通过自适应观测器进行运算。核心思想是利用过参数化模型解决未知系统阶数与观测器阶数不匹配的问题，并通过离散算法逐步学习模型阶数，同时用标准参数自适应律学习参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种遵循神经形态计算原则的模型识别框架，避免使用神经网络作为数学基础，解决连续时间系统中模型阶数未知带来的技术挑战。

Method: 采用过参数化模型作为输入-输出等价模型，处理过建模情况，并理论上扩展到欠建模情况；使用离散算法编排连续实验以逐步学习模型阶数，配合标准参数自适应律学习参数。

Result: 提出的框架能够在连续时间域中实现模型识别，有效处理未知系统阶数与观测器阶数的不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 过参数化模型是解决神经形态计算框架中模型阶数识别问题的关键概念，离散算法与参数自适应律的结合为连续时间系统识别提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We consider a problem to develop a framework for model identification adhering to the tenets of neuromorphic computation, without resorting to neural networks as the mathematical substrate. In particular, all computations take place in continuous time. We are naturally led to adaptive observers, where the main technical obstacle is the possible mismatch between the unknown plant order and the observer order. The key concept that informs the proposed framework is an overparameterized model, an input-output equivalent model that provides a suitable parameterization in the overmodeled case, with theoretical extensions also addressing the undermodeled case. A discrete algorithm orchestrates successive experiments to incrementally learn the model order, while a standard parameter adaptation law learns the parameters.

</details>


### [207] [Dominance and symmetry-breaking rules for the Graph Burning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10966)
*Nice Prado,Rafael Colares*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究图燃烧问题（GBP），通过与支配集问题的类比，提出新的整数线性规划公式，并应用支配规则、对称性破缺技术和目标函数扰动来减少搜索空间，加速求解过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图燃烧问题是NP难组合优化问题，现有整数线性规划方法在大图上收敛困难，因此需要减少搜索空间以提高求解性能。

Method: 通过研究GBP与支配集问题的相似性，提出新的公式化表达；应用支配规则和对称性破缺技术缩减搜索空间；引入目标函数扰动及对应的剪枝规则进一步加速求解。

Result: 所提方法有效减少了搜索空间，加速了求解时间。

Conclusion: 通过与支配集问题的类比及多种加速技术的结合，能够显著提升图燃烧问题的求解效率。

Abstract: The Graph Burning Problem (GBP) is a NP-Hard combinatorial optimization problem that models the propagation of influence or contagion in a network. The propagation is represented through the metaphor of a fire spreading through the vertices of a graph. A burning process takes place in a series of discrete time-steps. At each time step, the burning process is characterized by a propagation (where burned nodes spread the fire to their neighbors), and an ignition (where one additional unburned node is chosen to become burned). The minimum number of steps required to burn all vertices of a graph defines its burning number. Literature provides integer linear programming formulations to solve the problem, but with no surprise, such approaches struggle to converge as the graph size increases. Therefore, reducing the search space explored by these formulations becomes a key point to improve performances. In this work, we study the similarities of the Graph Burning Problem with the well-known Dominating Set Problem. We propose a new formulation based on this study and apply dominance rules and symmetry-breaking techniques to reduce the search space and consequently speed up resolution time. We also introduce a perturbation of the proposed objective function, as well as a pruning rule for the perturbed model in order to further accelerate its resolution.

</details>


### [208] [The Fuel Optimizer: A Data-Driven Numerical Framework for Formulation of Aviation Turbine Fuel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11099)
*Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Jacobo Porteiro,Dario Lopez-Pintor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向可持续航空燃料的反向设计框架Fuel Optimizer，通过从用户定义的效益函数出发，识别最优的化学成分或碳氢化合物组合。案例研究中，利用反应器模型模拟满足特定性能标准的燃料混合物在巡航条件下的污染物排放，建立代理模型以降低计算成本，并使用遗传算法优化燃料配方。优化后的燃料候选在打破氮氧化物与CO权衡以及最小化LTO循环污染物排放方面优于训练数据库，并通过反应器模拟验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种能够根据用户定义的效益函数自动寻找最优可持续航空燃料配方的反向设计方法，以同时优化多个目标（如污染物排放和燃料性能），解决传统燃料设计方法的局限性。

Method: 采用反向设计框架，包括：1）基于反应器模型模拟燃料混合物排放；2）构建代理模型替代高成本的反应器模拟；3）使用遗传算法优化燃料配方；4）约束条件包括性能标准（如密封膨胀）和成分限制。

Result: 优化后的燃料候选在所有效益函数上均优于训练数据库，最优候选通过反应器模拟得到了验证。

Conclusion: Fuel Optimizer框架能够有效优化可持续航空燃料配方，打破NOx与CO的权衡关系，并最小化LTO循环总污染物排放。

Abstract: The Fuel Optimizer is an inverse design framework for sustainable aviation fuels that starts from a user-defined merit function and identifies the optimal combination of chemical species or hydrocarbon families that optimize a combination of targets. As a case study, a database of fuel blends meeting selected property standards was simulated in a reactor model to obtain pollutant emissions at cruise conditions. A surrogate model was developed to reduce the computational cost of evaluating candidate blends, taking fuel composition as input and predicting emissions as output. A genetic algorithm was used to optimize fuel formulation according to two merit functions designed to break the nitrogen oxides - CO trade-off, and minimize pollutant emissions over the landing-and-take-off cycle. Constraints included selected property standards (including seal swelling) and composition limits. Fuel candidates from the framework outperformed the training database across all merit functions, and the optimal candidates were validated through reactor simulations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Business World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10044)
*Cecil Pang,Hiroki Sayama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了业务世界模型（BWM）的概念与架构，该模型借鉴AI、认知科学和控制理论中的世界模型思想，将业务状态、动态、约束、目标和可行动作空间进行编码，以支持自主决策。BWM整合语义数据表示、概率机器学习模型、确定性业务规则和显式动作空间，作为可执行业务内部模拟器，实现从指令执行向目标驱动规划与执行的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在商业中的应用多局限于自动化预定义任务，未能充分发挥其在战略层面的规划、优化和执行潜力。作者旨在建立一个能够从高层战略目标出发、自主规划并执行业务方案的智能系统基础框架。

Method: 采用业务语义中心化的形式化方法，将业务状态、动态和动作与关键业务实体关联；架构上整合语义数据表示、概率机器学习模型、确定性业务规则和显式动作空间，形成可执行的内部模拟器，支持行动序列模拟、未来结果评估及不确定性下的权衡分析。

Result: 提出BWM概念及其架构设计，建立了从指令执行向目标驱动规划与执行转变的概念基础；各组成组件虽非全新，但BWM的创新在于将其组织为面向业务方案的统一可执行内部模拟器。

Conclusion: BWM为构建能够自主执行业务方案的智能系统奠定了概念基础，推动了AI在商业领域从自动化工具向战略性自主决策系统的演进。

Abstract: Businesses are increasingly adopting AI-enabled tools to improve productivity, reduce costs, and enhance products and services. However, the transformative potential of AI extends beyond automating predefined tasks: it lies in enabling intelligent systems to plan, optimize, and execute business initiatives from high-level strategic objectives. This paper introduces the concept and architecture of a business world model (BWM), a world model specialized for business and organizational environments. Inspired by world models in artificial intelligence, cognitive science, and control theory, a BWM encodes business states, dynamics, constraints, objectives, and feasible action space to support autonomous decision-making. We propose a business-semantics-centric formulation in which business states, dynamics and actions are linked to key business entities. Within this framework, agents can simulate alternative action sequences, estimate their effects on future business outcomes, and evaluate trade-offs under uncertainty. The proposed architecture integrates semantic data representations, probabilistic machine learning models, deterministic business rules, and explicit action space into a coherent structure for planning and counterfactual reasoning. Although its individual components are not new, the contribution of BWM lies in organizing them as an executable internal simulator for business initiatives. This work establishes a conceptual foundation for autonomous business systems capable of moving from instruction-based execution toward goal-driven planning and execution.

</details>


### [210] [Deployment-Time Memorization in Foundation-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10062)
*Lei,Chen,Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao,Dalmo Cirne,Andy Olsen,Xu Chu,Zeke Miller,Alet Blanken,Amine Anoun,Jerry Ting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了部署时记忆（deployment-time memorization）问题，通过系统性地调整摘要攻击性、检索广度(k)和删除模式三个记忆设计旋钮，在隐私-效用前沿上衡量个性化召回率（PR）与对抗性提取率（AER），并引入遗忘残留分数（FRS）量化删除信息的可恢复性。实验表明，关键事实摘要可在几乎不损失个性化召回率的情况下显著降低提取率，但会引入删除保真度失败——仅原始删除仍可从派生摘要中恢复约20%的信息，仅完整管道清除或墓碑编辑可实现零残留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作关注参数记忆化或审计固定记忆配置，但未刻画记忆设计选择如何共同塑造个性化效用、提取风险与删除保真度三者之间的权衡关系。

Method: 将智能体记忆建模为隐私-效用前沿，测量个性化召回率(PR)和对抗性提取率(AER)，系统扫描三个记忆设计旋钮（摘要攻击性、检索广度k、删除模式），并提出遗忘残留分数(FRS)衡量删除信息的可恢复性。

Result: 关键事实摘要使Gemma 3 12B的提取率降低76%，GPT-4o-mini降低64%，同时几乎保持全部个性化召回率；但原始删除模式留下约20%的残留信息，仅完整管道清除或墓碑编辑可实现零残留。

Conclusion: 持久化智能体记忆必须作为一类一等记忆化机制进行评估——评估其帮助智能体回忆的内容、可提取的内容以及能够真正擦除的内容。

Abstract: Foundation-model agents are increasingly long-lived systems that remember users across interactions, making memorization an explicit deployment-time function rather than solely a property of model weights. Existing work addresses parametric memorization or audits fixed memory configurations, but does not characterize how memory-design choices jointly shape personalization utility, extraction risk, and deletion fidelity. We study this surface as deployment-time memorization, formulating agent memory as a privacy-utility frontier measured by Personalization Recall (PR) and Adversarial Extraction Rate (AER), and sweeping three memory-design knobs: summarization aggressiveness, retrieval breadth (k), and deletion mode. We further introduce the Forgetting Residue Score (FRS) to quantify whether deleted information remains recoverable from derived memory tiers. On LongMemEval, key-fact summarization reduces canary extraction by 76% on Gemma 3 12B and 64% on GPT-4o-mini while preserving nearly all personalization recall; critically, once content is compressed away, increasing k no longer restores leakage. The same compression, however, induces a deletion-fidelity failure: raw-only deletion leaves derived summary copies recoverable in approximately 20% of instances, and only full-pipeline purge or tombstone redaction drives worst-tier residue to zero. Together, these results establish that persistent agent memory must be evaluated as a first-class memorization mechanism -- assessed by what it helps agents recall, what it makes extractable, and what it can truly erase.

</details>


### [211] [Exploratory Responsiveness and Adaptive Rigidity under AI-Assisted Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10086)
*Balaraju Battu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个理论框架，研究AI辅助优化下的探索性适应机制，核心观点是AI的长期适应效应取决于预测性辅助与探索性响应之间的动态交互。通过构建动态模型，发现收敛性预测体制会降低自适应响应能力，导致元稳定陷阱、滞后、早熟收敛和探索崩溃等动力学现象，而探索增强体制则能放大探索搜索和适应性流动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对AI辅助优化下长期适应效应的系统性理论分析，未能揭示预测性辅助如何与探索性响应相互作用影响系统的自适应能力。

Method: 采用动力学框架，将认知、制度和技术系统建模为在崎岖认知景观上演化的动态系统，以自适应响应能力为核心状态变量，分析不同预测体制下的系统演化行为。

Result: 识别出两种典型动力学体制：收敛性预测体制下AI替代探索性参与，导致探索崩溃、滞后和局部高效但全局刚性的状态；探索增强体制下AI放大探索搜索和适应性流动性。有效替代参数依赖响应能力，低响应系统更易被替代，高响应系统可利用AI扩展探索。

Conclusion: AI的长期适应效应不仅取决于AI能力本身，还依赖于制度结构、发展背景和人机交互架构，为理解AI系统对自适应能力的差异化影响提供了理论框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a theory of exploratory adaptation under AI-assisted optimization. The central argument is that the long-run adaptive effects of AI systems depend critically on how predictive assistance interacts with exploratory responsiveness itself. We formalize this mechanism using a dynamical framework in which cognitive, institutional, and technological systems evolve over rugged epistemic landscapes characterized by multiple locally reinforced configurations. A central state variable in the model is adaptive responsiveness, which measures the capacity of a system to traverse unfamiliar conceptual and institutional trajectories under changing conditions. Under convergent predictive regimes, AI systems substitute for exploratory engagement, reducing adaptive responsiveness and generating metastable trapping, hysteresis, premature convergence, and exploration-collapse dynamics in which systems become locally efficient but globally rigid. The framework also identifies contrasting exploration-enhancing regimes in which AI systems amplify exploratory search, conceptual traversal, and adaptive mobility. The effective substitution parameter is therefore responsiveness-dependent: systems possessing weak exploratory routines are more vulnerable to exploratory substitution, whereas systems already possessing high adaptive responsiveness may use AI assistance to expand exploratory mobility across rugged landscapes. The long-run adaptive effects of AI consequently depend not only on AI capability itself, but also on institutional structure, developmental context, and the architecture of human-machine interaction.

</details>


### [212] [Predictive Assistance and the Temporal Dynamics of Exploratory Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10094)
*Balaraju Battu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何动力学框架，研究预测性AI系统如何通过外源性探索压缩改变人类的认知探索动力学，发现持续预测稳定会降低探索响应性、曲率不对称累积导致滞后效应，且早期干预会限制未来探索广度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典认知理论将问题解决描述为通过结构化问题空间的探索性搜索，而预测性AI系统引入了一种新机制：在探索性多样化展开之前就可能发生稳定化，从而在内部生成搜索之前就提供解决方案。这种转变对认知探索的长期影响尚不明确。

Method: 构建了一个几何动力学框架，其中注意力在由稳定漂移、内源性探索扰动和响应性门控学习塑造的策略景观上演化。将预测性辅助建模为外源性探索压缩过程，在自生探索拓宽策略空间可及区域之前稳定轨迹。

Result: 三个主要结果：(1)持续预测稳定通过衰减内源性扰动的有效影响来降低探索响应性；(2)曲率不对称累积和松弛，产生滞后效应和恢复延迟；(3)发展结果关键取决于稳定化时机，早期干预在表征多样化之前就限制了未来探索范围。

Conclusion: 预测性系统可能重塑探索性认知本身的几何结构，并产生了关于探索熵、过早收敛和预测稳定后延迟恢复的可检验预测。

Abstract: Classical theories of cognition describe problem solving as exploratory search through structured problem spaces in which repeated interaction gradually compresses search into efficient representational structures. Predictive artificial intelligence systems introduce a distinct regime in which stabilization may occur before exploratory diversification unfolds, supplying solutions and decision trajectories prior to internally generated search. This paper develops a geometric dynamical framework in which attention evolves over a landscape of strategies shaped by stabilizing drift, endogenous exploratory perturbation, and responsiveness-gated learning. Predictive assistance is modeled as a process of exogenous exploratory compression that stabilizes trajectories before self-generated exploration broadens the accessible regions of strategy space. The framework yields three main results. First, sustained predictive stabilization reduces exploratory responsiveness by attenuating the effective influence of intrinsic perturbations even when exploratory variability remains present. Second, curvature accumulates and relaxes asymmetrically, producing hysteresis and delayed recovery of exploratory mobility after assistance withdrawal. Third, developmental outcomes depend critically on the timing of stabilization, with early intervention narrowing future exploratory traversal before broad representational diversification has occurred. The framework generates empirically testable predictions concerning exploratory entropy, premature convergence, and delayed recovery following predictive stabilization.
